# DASHBOARD v5 pre-check — companion: the 102-panel wiring table

Companion to [`2026-09-06-v5-precheck.md`](2026-09-06-v5-precheck.md) §4.
Worktree base `e28b604d`; running rev `ea3f33fe` (identical Go/web code, §0).
Account names are redacted to «SIM-A» / «SIM-B» per A25.

---
## 4. SECTION C — THE PANEL-BY-PANEL READ

102 panels traced in render order, each: component → hook/client → endpoint → Go handler → store query or TCP frame → fields → cadence → failure render. Six agents, one per group; every line re-opened in the worktree at `e28b604d`.


### 4.1 G1 — identity, account, ledger, health, pause, emergency flat

*16 panels.*


**1. Owner pause banner (⏸ PAUSED until … CT)**

- **component** web/src/pages/TraderDashboardPage.tsx:426-434 (gated by isPauseActive(status?.stop_until), web/src/components/trader/PauseButton.tsx:36-40); CT render via pauseUntilCT PauseButton.tsx:42-49 (Intl tz America/Chicago)
- **hook/client** useSWR key `status-${selectedTraderId}${acctSuffix}` web/src/router/AppRoutes.tsx:318-326 -> api.getStatus web/src/lib/api/data.ts:29-41
- **endpoint** `GET /api/status?trader_id=<id>&account=<acct>`
- **handler** api/handler_order.go:113-128 handleStatus -> trader.GetStatus(); route registered api/server.go:383-386
- **store/frame** NO SQL. In-memory: trader/auto_trader_decision.go:91 "stop_until": pauseStatusString(at) -> trader/auto_trader_pause.go:122-127 -> at.PauseState(); armed by POST /traders/:id/pause (api/handler_trader.go:1002 tr.PauseEntriesUntil)
- **fields** status.stop_until (only). Rendered as the literal string '⏸ PAUSED until {pauseUntilCT(stop_until)} CT (owner) — new entries blocked; stops, targets and position management continue' (TraderDashboardPage.tsx:431-433)
- **cadence** 5000 ms — REFRESH_STATUS_MS web/src/lib/autoRefresh.ts:42, applied at AppRoutes.tsx:322; dedupingInterval 3000 (:324); revalidateOnFocus false (:323)
- **on failure** Renders STALE DATA SILENTLY. The status SWR has NO onErrorRetry/gate (unlike account/positions/decisions at AppRoutes.tsx:335,355,383) — on error SWR keeps the last `status` object and the banner keeps rendering the old stop_until. When status is undefined the optional chain (`status?.stop_until`) makes isPauseActive return false at PauseButton.tsx:37 -> banner renders BLANK (no error, no 'unknown').
- **notes** Unpaused renders the Go zero time '0001-01-01T00:00:00Z' (auto_trader_pause.go:126); Date.parse of that is a finite ms value far in the past so isPauseActive is false — correct, but the banner's absence is indistinguishable from 'status never loaded'.

- **hook/client** useSWR key 'traders-dashboard' AppRoutes.tsx:253-260 -> api.getTraders web/src/lib/api/traders.ts:20-27; selectedTrader picked at AppRoutes.tsx:412-414

- **fields** selectedTrader.trader_id (sliced to 8 chars + '...', :460); selectedTrader.trader_name — NOT rendered as text, used ONLY as avatar hash seed (:450 -> PunkAvatar.tsx:340-343 `${traderId}-${traderName}`)

TraderInfo declares startup_warning (types/trading.ts:121), custom_prompt(:125), use_ai500(:126), use_oi_top(:127), system_prompt_template(:128) — handleTraderList (handler_order.go:45-55) emits NONE of them; startup_warning production call sites: 0.

**3. Trader header — AI Model / Exchange / Strategy / Cycles / Runtime chips**

- **component** web/src/pages/TraderDashboardPage.tsx:558-614 (AI Model :559-578, Exchange :580-588, Strategy :590-595, Cycles :599-604, Runtime :606-611)
- **hook/client** traders SWR AppRoutes.tsx:253-260; exchanges SWR AppRoutes.tsx:262-269 -> api.getExchangeConfigs web/src/lib/api/config.ts:130-134; status SWR AppRoutes.tsx:318-326
- **endpoint** `GET /api/my-traders (model/exchange_id/strategy_name) + GET /api/exchanges (display name) + GET /api/status (cycles/runtime)`
- **handler** api/handler_order.go:16-59 handleTraderList; api/handler_exchange.go:126-154 handleGetExchangeConfigs (route api/server.go:261-264); api/handler_order.go:113-128 handleStatus
- **store/frame** SQL: store.Trader().List / store.Strategy().Get / store.Exchange().List(userID) (handler_exchange.go:129). Cycles+Runtime are IN-MEMORY process counters: trader/auto_trader_decision.go:85 "runtime_minutes": int(time.Since(at.startTime).Minutes()), :86 "call_count": at.callCount
- **fields** selectedTrader.ai_model (via getModelDisplayName after split('_').pop(), :573-576); exchange.exchange_type + exchange.account_name composed at :53-64 as 'TYPE - account_name'; selectedTrader.strategy_name || 'No Strategy' (:593); status.call_count (:602); status.runtime_minutes (:609)
- **cadence** AI Model / Strategy: 10000 ms (autoRefresh.ts:38). Exchange chip: 60000 ms — REFRESH_EXCHANGES_MS autoRefresh.ts:40 applied AppRoutes.tsx:266. Cycles/Runtime: 5000 ms (autoRefresh.ts:42).
- **on failure** Exchange chip degrades SILENTLY to a fabricated-looking label: getExchangeDisplayNameFromList returns 'Unknown' when exchange_id is absent (:57) or exchangeId.substring(0,8).toUpperCase()+'...' when the exchanges list has not loaded / does not contain it (:59) — the user sees a truncated UUID, not an error. Cycles/Runtime are wrapped in `{status && (...)}` (:596) -> render BLANK when status is undefined, and render STALE silently on a status error. Strategy renders the literal 'No Strategy' when strategy_name is empty — indistinguishable from a failed strategy lookup at handler_order.go:38 (the err is swallowed, strategyName stays "").
- **notes** runtime_minutes is time since trader start, so it resets to 0 on every deploy/restart — it is not an uptime of the strategy. The Exchange chip renders exchange.account_name, i.e. the exchange-config user label.

**4. PauseButton (⏸ Pause / ▶ Resume + menu)**

- **component** web/src/components/trader/PauseButton.tsx:51-158 (mounted TraderDashboardPage.tsx:467-477); testids pause-entries-button :101, resume-entries-button :87, pause-30m :114, pause-1h :122, pause-session-end :130, pause-custom-min :138, pause-custom-go :147
- **hook/client** api.pauseTrader web/src/lib/api/traders.ts:76-85; api.resumeTrader web/src/lib/api/traders.ts:87-92 (httpClient.post)
- **endpoint** `READ: none of its own (prop). WRITE: POST /api/traders/:id/pause  body {minutes} | {until:'session_end'} | {until_ct}; POST /api/traders/:id/resume`
- **handler** api/handler_trader.go:943-1011 handlePauseTrader; api/handler_trader.go:1013-1027 handleResumeTrader. Routes api/server.go:214-219
- **store/frame** NO SQL for the write itself: tr.PauseEntriesUntil(until,'owner') (handler_trader.go:1002) / tr.ResumeEntries('owner') (:1025) mutate in-process state in trader/auto_trader_pause.go (armed log :49, cleared :62, restored-after-restart :79, expiry :111). Ownership is checked against SQL first: s.store.Trader().GetFullConfig(userID, traderID) (:946, :1016) -> 404 on miss.
- **fields** Reads only the `stopUntil` prop (= status.stop_until). Renders one of two buttons: '▶ Resume' when isPauseActive (:84-96) else '⏸ Pause' + the 4-option menu (:98-157). Response fields message/pause_until/pause_until_ct (handler_trader.go:1006-1010) are DISCARDED by the client (traders.ts:84 returns void).
- **cadence** No poll of its own. Its rendered state follows the status SWR at 5000 ms (autoRefresh.ts:42). After a write it calls onChanged() -> TraderDashboardPage.tsx:470-476 mutate(predicate startsWith `status-${selectedTraderId}`), forcing an immediate status refetch.
- **on failure** SILENT. PauseButton.tsx:65-67 and :77-79 only `console.error` — no toast, no inline error, no state change; the button simply un-busies and still reads '⏸ Pause'. A 404 (not owner) or a 400 ('no active session to pause until', handler_trader.go:974) is invisible to the operator. Contrast handleClosePosition (TraderDashboardPage.tsx:302) which uses notify.error.
- **notes** pause_until_ct (handler_trader.go:1009) production call sites: 0 — the client returns Promise<void>. The custom-minutes input strips non-digits (:142) so only the {minutes} form and the two presets are reachable from the UI; the {until_ct:'HH:MM'} form (handler_trader.go:978-996) has NO UI producer — production call sites: 0.

**5. EmergencyFlatButton (red) + confirm modal**

- **component** web/src/components/trader/EmergencyFlatButton.tsx:19-123 (mounted TraderDashboardPage.tsx:480); testids emergency-flat-button :58, confirm-flat-modal :71, emergency-flat-result :91, cancel-flat-button :100, confirm-flat-button :109
- **hook/client** RAW fetch, NOT the api client — EmergencyFlatButton.tsx:29-38, with the JWT read straight from localStorage.getItem('auth_token') (:28)
- **endpoint** `POST /api/risk/force-flat?trader_id=<id>`
- **handler** api/handler_risk.go:78-158 handleForceFlat; route api/server.go:449-453; cross-user IDOR gate api/handler_plan.go:97-131 planTraderOwnership (mounted api/server.go:146, fires on the /api/risk/ prefix -> 404 via SafeNotFound :124)
- **store/frame** NO SQL. TCP path: ntTCP.CloseLong("",0) (handler_risk.go:106) -> the NT8 AddOn signal frame; then kernel.MaybeForceFlat with a nil-writer adapter (:117, return DISCARDED via `_ =`). Non-TCP CSV path: adapter at :145, kernel.MaybeForceFlat :146.
- **fields** The WHOLE response object, JSON.stringify(data,null,2) (:40) into a <pre> (:90-95). Response shape forceFlatResponse (handler_risk.go:65-72): triggered, trader_id, positions_flattened, timestamp_utc, log_message, reason.
- **cadence** None — one-shot on Confirm click (:112). No polling, no revalidation of any other surface after the flatten (it does NOT mutate the positions/account SWR keys).
- **on failure** WRONG-COLOURED SUCCESS. `r.ok` is never checked (:38-40): an HTTP 400 / 404 error body is stringified into the SAME <pre>, whose class is `text-green-300` (:92) — a refusal renders as green success text. Only a network throw takes the `Error: ${msg}` branch (:41-44), and that too renders green. The modal never closes on its own after a result (closeModal only fires on Cancel/backdrop, :49-53).
- **notes** handler_risk.go:121 PositionsFlattened: 1 is a HARDCODED LITERAL on the live TCP path — 'positions_flattened' counts nothing (the CSV path at :154 uses adapter.flattened, incremented exactly once at :55, so it is also always 1). Modal copy at :84-87 claims it 'triggers the kernel kill switch and resets the daily PnL window'; on the TCP path that reset's error return is discarded (handler_risk.go:117), so a failed reset is invisible to both the caller and the modal. Raw fetch bypasses httpClient's 401 interceptor — an expired token renders {"error":...} in green instead of logging out.

**6. AccountSelector (button + dropdown) and its masquerade warning**

- **component** web/src/components/trader/AccountSelector.tsx:20-250 (mounted TraderDashboardPage.tsx:483-508); trigger :141-170; masquerade banner :175-184; portal dropdown :187-247
- **hook/client** useSWR key `accounts-${traderId}` AccountSelector.tsx:30-45 -> api.getAccounts web/src/lib/api/traders.ts:157-163; write api.selectAccount traders.ts:165-182. The SAME key is fetched a second time in AppRoutes.tsx:310-314 (shared SWR cache).
- **endpoint** `READ: GET /api/accounts?trader_id=<id>. WRITE: POST /api/account/select?trader_id=<id> body {"account":"…"}`
- **handler** api/handler_account.go:43-104 handleGetAccounts; api/handler_account.go:109-217 handleSelectAccount. Routes api/server.go:603-611
- **store/frame** TCP FRAME + SQL. accounts/current from tcpServer.GetAccountsList() (handler_account.go:65) — the AddOn's accounts_list frame. `selected` from SQL s.store.Trader().GetByID(traderID).Account (:83-85). `current` is then OVERWRITTEN with `selected` when AccountStateFor(selected) has a snapshot (:93-97), i.e. when the account_balance frame (AccountBalancePayload, provider/ninjatrader/tcp_framing.go:284-291, read via tcp_server.go:533-541) exists for the bound account. Write persists via store.Trader().UpdateAccount (handler_account.go:206).
- **fields** accountsData.selected -> the button label 'Account: <selected>' (:154-157) or 'Select an account' (:158-162). accountsData.current -> the masquerade banner (:180). accounts[].name (:232) and accounts[].is_sim (:209,213,215,221,233) -> selectable vs greyed '(live — not selectable for auto-trade)'. Checkmark keyed on name===selected (:239-241).
- **cadence** NONE — the SWR config at AccountSelector.tsx:44 is `{revalidateOnFocus:false, dedupingInterval:5000}` with NO refreshInterval; AppRoutes.tsx:313 on the same key is likewise `{revalidateOnFocus:false, dedupingInterval:5000}`. Refetch happens only on mount, on traderId change (key change), and on the explicit mutate() after a successful select (:105).
- **on failure** Three explicit branches. isLoading -> a pulse skeleton chip (:117-126). SWR error -> a red bordered chip reading the hardcoded 'Failed to load accounts' (:128-137) — a real error state, not a zero. Success-but-empty accounts array -> the dropdown body renders 'Waiting for accounts...' (:200-203). A failed select renders notify.error with the server message (:107-110).
- **notes** handler_account.go:74 `IsCurrent: a.Name == current` is computed BEFORE the :93-97 override rewrites `current`, so the per-row is_current flags and the top-level `current` can disagree in the same response. Harmless today only because Account.is_current (web/src/types/trading.ts:44) has production call sites: 0. Because nothing polls this key, the masquerade banner can stay wrong indefinitely after the connection's streamed account moves.

**8. AI-402 provider-credit banner (AI CREDIT EXHAUSTED)**

- **component** web/src/pages/TraderDashboardPage.tsx:619-637 (testid ai-402-banner :621); polling effect :185-200
- **hook/client** planApi.getRiskErrors web/src/lib/api/plan.ts:588-599 (httpClient.request), called from a raw useEffect+setInterval at TraderDashboardPage.tsx:188-195
- **endpoint** `GET /api/risk/errors?trader_id=<id>`
- **handler** api/handler_gate_blocks.go:63-73 handleErrors; route api/server.go:469-473
- **store/frame** NO SQL — an IN-MEMORY map. telemetry.ErrorSummary (telemetry/errors.go:117-132) over telemetry.errorEvents (:49), keyed by {trader,type,cause,cost} (:32-37). Rows are written by telemetry.RecordError (:73-102), whose 402 call site is trader/auto_trader_loop.go:625-626 (cost CostDecisionLost), classified by classifyAIError trader/auto_trader_ai402.go:31-40.
- **fields** row.cause is stored into state (:192) but NEVER rendered. Only ai402.count is rendered, inside the fixed string 'AI CREDIT EXHAUSTED — provider returned HTTP 402 (Insufficient Balance); decision cycles are failing ({count} this session). Top up, then the banner clears on the first successful call.' (:631-634)
- **cadence** 30000 ms — setInterval(poll, 30_000) TraderDashboardPage.tsx:195, plus one immediate poll at :194; effect keyed on selectedTraderId (:200), skipped entirely when selectedTraderId is falsy (:186)
- **on failure** UNGUARDED. The poll at :188-193 has NO try/catch: httpClient.request rethrows network errors (web/src/lib/httpClient.ts:258), so a network failure rejects inside `void poll()` as an unhandled rejection and setAi402 is never called — the banner keeps its PREVIOUS state (stale) and renders BLANK if it was never set. On an HTTP error status, request() returns {success:false} with no `data`, res is that object, res?.rows is undefined, `.find` is never reached, and setAi402(null) HIDES the banner — a backend error silently reads as 'no 402'.
- **notes** TWO defects. (a) The banner's own text (:633-634) and the code comment (:181) claim it 'clears on the first successful call'. FALSE for THIS banner: telemetry/errors.go has NO decrement or clear path; the only reset is SetErrorSessionDay (:60-68), called from trader/auto_trader_loop.go:256 at the 17:00 CT CME rollover — so the banner persists until the session rollover or a process restart. The auto-ack at trader/auto_trader_ai402.go:71-77 acks store.Alert().AckByEvent — the ALERTS table, a different surface this banner never reads. (b) count under-reports: :191 `.find(r => r.type==='ai_payment_402')` takes ONE row from a list sorted by count desc (telemetry/errors.go:130); distinct 402 `cause` strings become separate rows (key includes cause, :34), so the banner shows the largest single cause's count, not the session total. Fields cause/cost/decisions_lost/trades_lost declared at plan.ts:592-596 — production call sites: 0.

**9. Debug strip left — SYSTEM_STATUS :: ONLINE · REV::<rev>**

- **component** web/src/pages/TraderDashboardPage.tsx:640-658 (colour ternary :642-649, text :651-657); polling effect :163-177
- **hook/client** RAW fetch('/api/health') at TraderDashboardPage.tsx:166 — not the api client, no auth header
- **endpoint** `GET /api/health (UNAUTHENTICATED — api/server.go:92 api.Any("/health", s.handleHealth))`
- **handler** api/server.go:626-632 handleHealth
- **store/frame** NO SQL, NO frame. status is the LITERAL string "ok" (api/server.go:628). revision from kernel.RunningRevision() (:630) -> kernel/boot_integrity.go:197-202, an atomic.Value set at boot, shortened to 12 chars by shortRev (:204-208).
- **fields** health.status -> 'ONLINE' when 'ok', else status.toUpperCase() (:652-656); health.revision -> ' · REV::<rev>' (:657). Colour: grey #8C8C8C while undefined, green #0ECB81 when ok, red #F6465D otherwise (:644-648).
- **cadence** 30000 ms — setInterval(poll, 30_000) TraderDashboardPage.tsx:172, plus one immediate poll at :171; effect deps [] (:177) so it runs once per mount for the page's lifetime
- **on failure** HONEST failure state: the .catch at :169 sets {status:'unreachable'}, which renders red 'SYSTEM_STATUS::UNREACHABLE' with no REV suffix (revision undefined -> :657 emits ''). Before the first response it renders grey 'SYSTEM_STATUS::…' — distinct from both ok and failed. This is the one panel in the group with a correct three-state failure render.
- **notes** 'ONLINE' proves only that gin answered — status is a hardcoded literal (server.go:628) and reads NOTHING about the NT8 link, feed_status, AI provider or DB. The response's third field `time` (server.go:629) resolves from c.Request.Context().Value("time"); no WithValue exists anywhere in api/ or main.go, and the live endpoint returns {"revision":"ea3f33fe9ccc","status":"ok","time":null} [A, curl 127.0.0.1:8080] — permanently null, production call sites: 0. GET /api/status also carries a `revision` field (trader/auto_trader_decision.go:96) that nothing reads and that is not in the SystemStatus contract (web/src/types/trading.ts:1-16) — production call sites: 0.

**10. Debug strip right — LAST_UPDATE / EQ / PNL**

- **component** web/src/pages/TraderDashboardPage.tsx:659-679 (LAST_UPDATE :661, EQ :662, PNL :663-665)
- **hook/client** useSWR key `account-${selectedTraderId}${acctSuffix}` AppRoutes.tsx:328-346 -> api.getAccount web/src/lib/api/data.ts:43-55
- **endpoint** `GET /api/account?trader_id=<id>&account=<acct>`
- **handler** api/handler_order.go:131-179 handleAccount; account map built at trader/auto_trader_decision.go:117-231; route api/server.go:387-390
- **store/frame** TCP FRAME, no SQL. handler_order.go:145 trader.GetAccountInfo() -> trader/auto_trader_decision.go:118 at.trader.GetBalance() -> trader/ninjatrader/tcp_trader.go:792 server.AccountStateFor(t.boundAccount) (fallback AccountState() :794) -> the account_balance frame AccountBalancePayload (provider/ninjatrader/tcp_framing.go:284-291), cached in TCPServer.acctBalances (tcp_server.go:533-541). total_equity = NetLiquidation, else CashValue+UnrealizedPnL (tcp_trader.go:806-809; auto_trader_decision.go:140-145). total_pnl = RealizedPnL+UnrealizedPnL because brokerNativePnL is true (tcp_trader.go:828-829 -> auto_trader_decision.go:197-206).
- **fields** lastUpdate (a client-side string, NOT from the API); account.total_equity.toFixed(2); account.total_pnl.toFixed(2) with title 'NT8-native total P&L (equity − initial balance)'
- **cadence** 5000 ms — REFRESH_ACCOUNT_MS autoRefresh.ts:44, applied AppRoutes.tsx:332 as `accountGate.off ? 0 : REFRESH_ACCOUNT_MS`; polling latches OFF after 2 failed retries (AppRoutes.tsx:335-341) and AUTO-RESUMES after REFRESH_BACKOFF_RESUME_MS = 30000 (autoRefresh.ts:62, usePollResume :84-91). lastUpdate is recomputed in a useEffect on each new `account` object (AppRoutes.tsx:406-410).
- **on failure** RENDERS STALE DATA SILENTLY. The branch order is `account ? (numbers) : accountFailed ? (red 'DATA_FETCH::FAILED — Account data unavailable, check connection', translations.ts:1210) : (three pulse skeletons)` (:659-678). SWR retains the last successful `account` across errors, so once ANY fetch has succeeded the red failure text is UNREACHABLE — the old EQ/PNL keep rendering, unlabelled, while polling is latched off for 30s at a time.
- **notes** The PNL tooltip (:663) says 'equity − initial balance', but on NT8 total_pnl is broker-native realized+unrealized (auto_trader_decision.go:197-206) — the tooltip describes the crypto branch (:182), not the futures branch that actually runs. LAST_UPDATE resolves from AppRoutes.tsx:408 `new Date().toLocaleTimeString()` — BROWSER-LOCAL time, unlabelled, sitting beside CT-pinned surfaces (PauseButton.tsx:42-49 pins America/Chicago).

**11. LEDGER_DAY chip (inside the debug strip)**

- **component** web/src/components/trader/LedgerDayPnl.tsx:28-46 (label builder ledgerDayLabel :10-26), mounted TraderDashboardPage.tsx:666; testid ledger-day-pnl LedgerDayPnl.tsx:32
- **hook/client** Same account SWR as panel 10 (AppRoutes.tsx:328-346); the component takes `account` as a prop, it fetches nothing
- **endpoint** `GET /api/account?trader_id=<id>&account=<acct>  (same response as panel 10)`
- **handler** api/handler_order.go:156-170 — the ledger block inside handleAccount; the query call is handler_order.go:159
- **store/frame** SQL. store/pnl_surface_guard.go:63-85 GetLedgerDayTotal — `WHERE trader_id=? AND status='CLOSED' AND exit_time>=? AND exit_time<? AND close_reason NOT IN ('reconcile_flat','unresolved','e7_farside_test')` (:66-67), plus `AND account = ?` when the ?account= param is non-empty (:68-70), summed via TraderPosition.CorrectedPnL (store/position.go:953-958, strict pnl_corrected; NULL -> Unresolved++ and excluded, pnl_surface_guard.go:75-81). Window is the CT calendar day computed at handler_order.go:157-158 (kernel.CTLocation).
- **fields** account.ledger_day_pnl, ledger_day_resolved, ledger_day_unresolved, ledger_day_status — composed at LedgerDayPnl.tsx:19-25 into 'LEDGER_DAY::+<total> (<n> resolved, <n> unresolved excluded)'. Colour green/red on sign, var(--vl-faint) when the total is not a number (:36-40).
- **cadence** 5000 ms — inherits the account SWR (autoRefresh.ts:44 / AppRoutes.tsx:332), including the latch-off-then-30s-auto-resume behaviour
- **on failure** CORRECT — no fabricated zero. If the whole account fetch fails, the chip is inside the `account ?` branch at TraderDashboardPage.tsx:659 and therefore renders STALE with the rest of the strip.

For TODAY (CT window 1788670800000-1788757200000) the query returns ZERO rows, so the chip renders 'LEDGER_DAY::+0.00 (0 resolved, 0 unresolved excluded)' — a COMPUTED zero carrying its own n, which is correct under A24. **12. StatCard — Total Equity**

- **component** web/src/pages/TraderDashboardPage.tsx:683-695 inside the grid at :682; StatCard body :1241-1307 (value :1276-1278, change ▲/▼ :1285-1297, loading skeleton :1268-1272)
- **hook/client** account SWR AppRoutes.tsx:328-346 -> api.getAccount data.ts:43-55
- **endpoint** `GET /api/account?trader_id=<id>&account=<acct>`
- **handler** api/handler_order.go:131-179 handleAccount (value produced at :145)
- **store/frame** account_balance TCP frame -> tcp_trader.go:806-809 (NetLiquidation, else CashValue+UnrealizedPnL) -> auto_trader_decision.go:140-145 totalEquity -> return map key "total_equity" (auto_trader_decision.go:215)
- **fields** account.total_equity.toFixed(2); unit = currencyUnit, which is 'USD' on ninjatrader and 'USDT' otherwise (TraderDashboardPage.tsx:241-243); change = account.total_pnl_pct || 0 (:691); positive = (account.total_pnl ?? 0) > 0 (:692)
- **cadence** 5000 ms (REFRESH_ACCOUNT_MS autoRefresh.ts:44 via AppRoutes.tsx:332); 0 while accountGate.off is latched, auto-resuming after 30000 ms (autoRefresh.ts:62)
- **on failure** THREE-WAY, and the middle case is the trap. loading={!account && !accountFailed} (:694) -> pulse skeleton. accountFailed && !account (:686) -> renders the literal '--'. accountFailed && account (the common case after any prior success) -> renders the LAST GOOD NUMBER with no indicator at all, because SWR keeps the cached data; polling is off, so the figure can be arbitrarily old.
- **notes** PLAUSIBLE ZERO: when no account_balance frame has arrived, tcp_trader.go:832-835 returns totalEquity 0.0 — whose stated intent (tcp_trader.go:779-781) is 'report zeros so the dashboard shows "no data" rather than a fabricated balance'. The dashboard has NO such branch: it renders '0.00 USD' identically to a real zero-equity account. The ▲/▼ on this card is NOT an equity change — it is total_pnl_pct (:691), which on NT8 is broker-native pnl/(equity−pnl) (auto_trader_decision.go:201-205). `|| 0` at :691 coerces a missing pct to a rendered '+0.00%'. positive uses `> 0` here but `>= 0` on the P&L card (:721) off the SAME field: total_pnl exactly 0 renders red ▼ here and green ▲ there.

- **hook/client** account SWR AppRoutes.tsx:328-346

- **component** panel JSX web/src/components/charts/AdvancedChart.tsx:1433-1500 (list map :1465-1489), toggle button :1402-1414; draw fn updateIndicators :1166-1234; config array :158-206
- **hook/client** web/src/utils/indicators.ts — calculateSMA :13-31, calculateEMA :34-56, calculateBollingerBands :66-97
- **endpoint** `NONE for the math — computed CLIENT-SIDE from the /api/klines candles already on the chart`
- **handler** NONE — no Go handler exists for chart MA/EMA/BB. searched: grep -rn 'calculateSMA|calculateEMA|calculateBollingerBands' across web/src and api/ — only web/src/utils/indicators.ts:13,:34,:66 and AdvancedChart.tsx:1180,:1189,:1199
- **store/frame** latestKlinesRef (AdvancedChart.tsx:136, filled :763) — the same array the candles use
- **fields** one LineSeries per enabled indicator with title = indicator.name (:1184,:1193) — MA5/MA10/MA20/MA60 (periods 5/10/20/60, :168-189), EMA12/EMA26 (dashed, lineStyle 2, :1194), BB Upper/Middle/Lower (:1204,:1214,:1223). Panel rows render indicator.color swatch (:1480) + indicator.name (:1483) + a ● when enabled (:1486)
- **cadence** redrawn on every 5 s data refresh via updateIndicators(klineData) at AdvancedChart.tsx:827, and immediately on toggle by the effect at :1278-1284 (dep [indicators]). Reads indicatorsRef (:211-212) so the 5 s stale closure cannot clear a just-toggled indicator (:207-210)
- **on failure** CRASH PATH: calculateEMA indexes data[i] for i<period with no length guard (indicators.ts:43-47). With fewer bars than the period (cold NT8 cache + EMA26 enabled) it throws inside loadData's try, is caught at AdvancedChart.tsx:1026 and rendered as the red error overlay carrying a raw JS TypeError string (:1640). calculateSMA/BB with period>length silently return [] → the line simply does not draw
- **notes** PERIODS ARE HARDCODED FE-SIDE (AdvancedChart.tsx:168,:174,:180,:189,:196,:203; BB defaults period=20/stdDev=2 at indicators.ts:68-69) and are NOT the strategy's configured IndicatorPeriods — the chart's MA/EMA/BB can therefore disagree with the periods the AI prompt is built from. All indicators default enabled:false except Volume (:159).

- **component** toggle row inside the indicator panel (AdvancedChart.tsx:162, id 'svp', default enabled:false); fetch AdvancedChart.tsx:1241-1251; attach/update/detach syncSVP :1253-1273; primitive web/src/components/charts/primitives/SessionVolumeProfile.ts (class :411, setData :467-500, pane update :282-396, renderer :116-...)
- **hook/client** fetchSVP (AdvancedChart.tsx:1241) via httpClient.request({silent:true}) (:1245); guarded by `if (exchange !== 'ninjatrader') return null` (:1242)
- **endpoint** `GET /api/klines/svp?symbol=&interval=&exchange= (AdvancedChart.tsx:1244)`
- **handler** s.handleKlinesSVP — registered api/server.go:157 (JWT-PROTECTED); impl api/handler_svp.go:31-63
- **store/frame** NO SQL. market.FuturesBarsProvider(symbol, interval, 2000) (api/handler_svp.go:56) → the same NT8 BarCache; profile computed by kernel.BuildSVPProfile(bars, time.Now()) (api/handler_svp.go:62, impl kernel/svp.go:304+, which drops any bar with CloseTime >= now, :324-325)
- **fields** per session: bins[].price → y (SessionVolumeProfile.ts:358), bins[].vol → bar width (:371), bins[].upVol → teal fraction (:374), poc → POC row flag (:380) + POC line (:389) + axis label `POC <n>` (:562), vah → :390 + `VAH <n>` (:568), val → :391 + `VAL <n>` (:574), rowHeight → row thickness (:363-368), sessionStart → x anchor (:315)
- **cadence** void syncSVP() on every 5 s data refresh (AdvancedChart.tsx:832, inside loadData) AND on indicator toggle (:1283). Attach happens once (:1268-1270); later polls only setData (:1272) so drawings never stack
- **on failure** SILENT NO-OP in every failure mode: !res.success || !res.data → return null (AdvancedChart.tsx:1246) and syncSVP returns at :1265 without touching the primitive → the LAST profile stays drawn (stale, not blank). Server side never errors: non-futures symbol, unbound provider, or zero bars all return a well-formed zero-value 200 `{rowHeight, sessions:[]}` (api/handler_svp.go:39-60). An off-screen session anchor is skipped, never thrown (SessionVolumeProfile.ts:322)
- **notes** A29 DEAD CONTRACT FIELDS — declared in SessionVolumeProfile.ts and emitted by kernel/svp.go but read by nobody: inVA (TS :44 / Go kernel/svp.go:70) production call sites: 0; downVol (TS :43 / Go :69) 0; date (TS :51 / Go :80) 0; partial (TS :52 / Go :81) 0; frozen (TS :57 / Go :86) 0. Verified by grep across web/src — the only hits are the interface declarations. Also: handler_svp.go:51 calls 2000 'the cache cap' but the cap is 2500 (provider/ninjatrader/bar_cache.go:24), and SVP profiles 2000 bars while the chart shows at most 1500.

**11. Order markers (B / S circles) + B/S toggle**

- **component** marker build AdvancedChart.tsx:834-1012 (markers pushed :932-955, applied :984-993); toggle button :1416-1428; visibility-only effect :1146-1163
- **hook/client** fetchPositionMarkers AdvancedChart.tsx:457-521 (chosen at :840-843 by `exchange === 'ninjatrader'`); fetchOrders :342-448
- **endpoint** `NT8 path: GET /api/positions/history?trader_id=&limit=200 (AdvancedChart.tsx:463). Every other exchange: GET /api/orders?trader_id=&symbol=&status=FILLED&limit=200 (:355)`
- **handler** NT8: s.handlePositionHistory — registered api/server.go:395, impl api/handler_order.go:205-256. Other: s.handleOrders — registered api/server.go:421, impl api/handler_order.go:314-356
- **store/frame** store.Position().GetClosedPositions(traderID, limit, c.Query("account")) — store/position.go:786-805: `WHERE trader_id = ? AND status = 'CLOSED'` [+ `AND account = ?` only when the param is non-empty] ORDER BY exit_time DESC LIMIT n. Row shape store/position.go:164-205
- **fields** from each row: symbol (AdvancedChart.tsx:471), side→long/short (:473), entry_price/entry_time → an 'open' marker (:477-487), exit_price/exit_time → a 'close' marker when status==='CLOSED' (:492-504). Drawn as circle markers: green 'B'/'Bn' belowBar (:935-942) and red 'S'/'Sn' aboveBar (:946-953), counts grouped per candle by binary search (:869-887,:914-919)
- **cadence** rebuilt inside loadData on every 5 s tick (AdvancedChart.tsx:840, REFRESH_CHART_MS=5000, autoRefresh.ts:52). Toggling B/S only re-applies the cached array (:1150-1153), no fetch
- **on failure** empty/failed fetch → catch returns [] (:518-519) → the `else` at :1003 clears markers to [] — chart keeps candles, markers vanish, only a console.log (:1004). An order outside the kline time range is dropped with a console.warn (:900-906), invisible in the UI
- **notes** THREE CONFIRMED DEFECTS. (a) CROSS-ACCOUNT LEAK: the FE never sends &account= (AdvancedChart.tsx:463) so handlePositionHistory's c.Query("account") is "" and GetClosedPositions skips the account filter (store/position.go:789-791). Measured on data/data.db: of the top 200 CLOSED rows by exit_time, n=200 → 127 are «SIM-A» (ids 393..591) and 73 are «SIM-B» (ids 382..519); those 73 render on the chart regardless of the selected account. (b) OPEN POSITIONS ARE NEVER MARKED: the store hard-filters status='CLOSED' (store/position.go:788), so the FE's entry-marker branch (:479) can never fire for a live position. Store totals: 587 CLOSED rows (512 «SIM-A» ids 1..591 + 75 «SIM-B» ids 382..519), 0 OPEN. (c) pnl is declared on OrderMarker (AdvancedChart.tsx:41) and never set by either fetcher and never rendered — production call sites: 0. Time parsing is correct: entry_time/exit_time are Unix MILLISECONDS (store/position.go:179,:182) and parseCustomTime converts >1e12 to seconds (AdvancedChart.tsx:268-280).

- **component** web/src/components/charts/AdvancedChart.tsx:1050-1143 — loadOpenOrders :1054-1123, line creation :1101-1108, priceLinesRef :135
- **hook/client** fetchOpenOrders AdvancedChart.tsx:524-552, httpClient.request({silent:true}) (:537)

- **handler** s.handleOpenOrders — registered api/server.go:427, impl api/handler_order.go:416-447 → trader.GetOpenOrders(symbol) after market.Normalize (:437, market/data.go:670-674 early-returns CME roots untouched)
- **store/frame** NT8: TCPTrader.GetOpenOrders (trader/ninjatrader/tcp_trader.go:1195-1207) → openOrdersSrc, wired to AutoTrader.ledgerOpenOrders at trader/auto_trader.go:803 → store.ArmedOrders().ListNonTerminal (store/armed_orders.go:363-373: `WHERE trader_id = ? AND state IN ('armed','working','cancel_pending') ORDER BY id`). Mapped to types.OpenOrder at trader/class33_boot_sweep.go:163-167
- **fields** per order: linePrice = stop_price>0 ? stop_price : price (AdvancedChart.tsx:1072-1073); colour + title — red `SL <qty>` (:1089-1090), green `TP <qty>` (:1092-1093), yellow `Limit <side> <qty>` (:1095-1096), else `<type> <qty>` (:1098). Dashed, axisLabelVisible (:1105-1106). types.OpenOrder JSON: order_id/symbol/side/position_side/type/price/stop_price/quantity/status/source (trader/types/interface.go:109-122)
- **cadence** initial setTimeout 1000 ms (AdvancedChart.tsx:1126) then setInterval REFRESH_OPEN_ORDERS_MS = 60000 ms (:1131-1137; constant web/src/lib/autoRefresh.ts:54), skipped while hidden or in flight (:1132). Effect deps [symbol, traderID] (:1143) — NOT interval
- **on failure** SILENT BLANK. Old lines are removed FIRST (:1057-1064), then any failure path leaves zero lines: !result.success → console.warn + [] (:542-544); a thrown error → catch console.error (:1121). Even the hard 500 case (GetOpenOrders refusing when the source is unwired, tcp_trader.go:1199-1201 → SafeInternalError, api/handler_order.go:442) is converted by httpClient to {success:false} (web/src/lib/httpClient.ts:189-191,:246-254) and rendered as NO LINES — indistinguishable from 'genuinely flat'
- **notes** MISPLACED/MISLABELLED LINE: for a resting limit entry ledgerOpenOrders emits Type='LIMIT', Price=EntryPx, StopPrice=StopPx (class33_boot_sweep.go:155-165). The FE prefers stop_price (:1073) but classifies by type — 'LIMIT'.includes('STOP') is false — so it draws ONE yellow line at the PROTECTIVE STOP price labelled `Limit BUY 1`, and the entry price is never drawn. MEASURED NOW: armed_orders rows in ('armed','working','cancel_pending') = 0 (states present: cancelled 51, filled 10, superseded 6) → this panel currently renders nothing at all. Also: ledgerOpenOrders IGNORES its symbol argument entirely (class33_boot_sweep.go:137-168 uses at.futuresSymbol()), so the ?symbol= filter is not honoured. The NT8 broker order book (nt8_order_snapshots, store/nt8_order_snapshot.go:39) is NOT read by this panel — production call sites for this path: 0.

ORDER BY timestamp DESC LIMIT 10000`, then reversed to ascending (:105-107); account=="" falls back to GetLatest (store/equity.go:70-86). Table trader_equity_snapshots (store/equity.go:33)

- **store/frame** NOT SQL for the number itself: AutoTrader.GetAccountInfo (trader/auto_trader_decision.go:117-231) reads at.trader.GetBalance() and takes balance["totalEquity"] when >0 (:140-141), else wallet+unrealized (:144); returned as "total_equity" (:215). For NT8 that is the account_balance TCP frame. handleAccount additionally attaches ledger_day_pnl etc. from store.Position().GetLedgerDayTotal (api/handler_order.go:159-168)
- **fields** `account?.total_equity.toFixed(2) || '0.00'` (EquityChart.tsx:275) + currencyLabel USD|USDT (:280, from isFutures at :46 ← TraderDashboardPage.tsx:241-242 exchange_type==='ninjatrader')

If account resolves but total_equity is absent, `.toFixed` throws (no `?.` after total_equity)
- **notes** TWO SOURCES FOR ONE CONCEPT: this header shows the LIVE TCP account frame (15 s), while the footer 'Current Equity' tile (:479) shows the newest DB snapshot (30 s). MEASURED: newest trader_equity_snapshots row for account «SIM-A» is id 37888 @ 2026-09-04 18:27:48Z with total_equity 51906.5 — i.e. the DB series is ~2 days stale as of 2026-09-06, so the two numbers can differ with no on-screen indication. ledger_day_pnl / ledger_day_resolved / ledger_day_unresolved / ledger_day_date / ledger_day_source (api/handler_order.go:164-168) are read by this component: production call sites 0.

*23 panels.*


**1. Session timeline strip (24h bands + killzones + now-marker + DST warning)**

- **component** web/src/components/plan/SessionTimelineStrip.tsx:25 (mounted web/src/components/plan/PlanCard.tsx:84)
- **hook/client** no client of its own. `activeSession` prop threaded from PlanCard.tsx:59 `const activeSession = (plan?.active_session as SessionName) || null`, whose plan comes from usePlanToday (web/src/components/plan/usePlan.ts:17) → api.getPlanToday (web/src/lib/api/plan.ts:257)
- **endpoint** `GET /api/plan/today — for the `activeSession` prop ONLY. Bands/killzones/enabled/now-marker/DST are LOCAL, no endpoint.`
- **handler** api/handler_plan.go:288 handlePlanToday; `active_session` written at api/handler_plan.go:357 (found=false branch) and api/handler_plan.go:486 (found=true branch), resolved by reg.ActiveSession at api/handler_plan.go:305 → kernel/session_registry.go:201
- **store/frame** active_session: system_config key `session_registry` read by planRegistry api/handler_plan.go:1983 (kernel/session_registry.go:25 SessionRegistryConfigKey), fail-safe to DefaultSessionRegistry. Everything else the strip draws comes from the FE constant SESSION_BANDS web/src/components/plan/sessionConfig.ts:41-72 — no SQL, no frame.
- **fields** band.name / band.startMin / band.endMin / band.hue / band.enabled (sessionConfig.ts:41-72) at SessionTimelineStrip.tsx:65-85; band.killzones at :88-105; nowCTMinutes(client clock) at :112 via sessionConfig.ts:78; londonDSTWarning at :37,:151 via sessionConfig.ts:118; legend labels via tp('sessionAsia'|'sessionLondon'|'sessionNY') at :126-149; activeSession only changes opacity/border (:76,:135,:141) and the aria-label (:52)
- **cadence** local re-render every 30_000 ms — SessionTimelineStrip.tsx:30 `setInterval(() => setTick(t => t + 1), 30_000)` (no fetch). The activeSession prop refreshes with the plan poll: 15_000 ms, usePlan.ts:13 PLAN_REFRESH_MS + usePlan.ts:34 refreshInterval
- **on failure** renders the full strip unchanged. A failed /plan/today makes getPlanToday return null (plan.ts:274) → activeSession null → NO band highlighted, NO error, nothing says the session state is unknown (SessionTimelineStrip.tsx:70-84, :135, :141). Never blank, never an error — silently indistinguishable from 'no session is live'.
- **notes** [A] the strip's enable shading is a CLIENT MIRROR of the Go registry (documented sessionConfig.ts:1-7,40). sessionConfig.ts:49 ASIA.enabled=false and :59 LONDON.enabled=false match the STORED registry (system_config `session_registry`: ASIA enabled=false, LONDON enabled=false, NY true), but the live strategy overrides both — strategies.id=a5b7662e-7bf7-49bb-9f09-7efa48f95ac8 day_plan.sessions=[{NY,replan_cap:4},{ASIA,enable:true,replan_cap:4},{LONDON,enable:true,replan_cap:4}] — and sessionRunnable lets the explicit override beat the registry flag (trader/auto_trader_planconfig.go:108-113). So ASIA/LONDON paint at the 'not enabled' opacity 0.1 (SessionTimelineStrip.tsx:76) on days the bot runs them. Corroborating rows: plans plan_id 2026-09-03:ASIA:8d5c8af5_… v1..v7 (n=7) and 2026-09-04:LONDON:8d5c8af5_… v1..v3 (n=3). The tabs (server-fed) are correct; only the strip is wrong.

- **component** web/src/components/plan/SessionTabs.tsx:25; states computed by computeSessionTabs web/src/components/plan/PlanCard.tsx:186-199; mounted PlanCard.tsx:85-90
- **hook/client** usePlanToday web/src/components/plan/usePlan.ts:17-40 → api.getPlanToday web/src/lib/api/plan.ts:257-275 (api aggregate: web/src/lib/api/index.ts:14 spreads planApi; API_BASE='/api' web/src/lib/api/helpers.ts:4)
- **endpoint** `GET /api/plan/today?trader_id=<id>[&symbol=][&session=][&version=] — web/src/lib/api/plan.ts:271`
- **handler** api/handler_plan.go:288 handlePlanToday, registered api/server.go:489-493 on the `protected` group with the IDOR guard api/server.go:146 → planTraderOwnership api/handler_plan.go:97-131
- **store/frame** `runnable_sessions` api/handler_plan.go:315-318 ← at.RunnableSessions() trader/auto_trader_planconfig.go:249-258 ← sessionRunnable trader/auto_trader_planconfig.go:104-121, which reads the registry (system_config `session_registry`, kernel/session_registry.go:189/201) AND the strategy day_plan block (strategies.config JSON, day_plan.sessions[].enable / sessions_enabled). `active_session` ← kernel/session_registry.go:201. NO query against `plans` for tab state.
- **fields** runnable_sessions[] and active_session (both consumed at PlanCard.tsx:59,:71); per tab {name, state} rendered SessionTabs.tsx:61-135 — aria-selected, aria-disabled, gold underline (:85), pulse dot when state==='active' (:99-111), title = tp('tabNight') when disabled (:93), label = tp('sessionAsia'|'sessionLondon'|'sessionNY') (:26-34,:124)
- **cadence** 15_000 ms — usePlan.ts:13 `PLAN_REFRESH_MS = 15_000`, applied usePlan.ts:34 `refreshInterval: version ? 0 : PLAN_REFRESH_MS`; dedupingInterval 5000 (usePlan.ts:36); revalidateOnFocus false (usePlan.ts:35). Pinning a historical ?version= sets the interval to 0 (usePlan.ts:34).
- **on failure** an HTTP error response resolves to {success:false} (web/src/lib/httpClient.ts:246-254) → getPlanToday returns null (plan.ts:274) → plan null → `plan?.runnable_sessions` undefined → computeSessionTabs falls back to the SESSION_BANDS constant (PlanCard.tsx:191-193), so ASIA and LONDON render DISABLED and refuse the click (SessionTabs.tsx:63,:79) with the tooltip 'night' — a silent, wrong, un-clickable state, not an error. A pure NETWORK error throws (httpClient.ts:258) → SWR keeps the last data → tabs render STALE silently.
- **notes** A29: TabState 'reading' (SessionTabs.tsx:11, branches at :64, :112-123, :125-132) has production call sites: 0 — computeSessionTabs (PlanCard.tsx:194-197) emits only active|inactive|disabled; grep over web/src (non-test) finds no other producer. The i18n key tabReading (web/src/i18n/plan-translations.ts:346) exists but is only reachable via the disabled-tooltip ternary.

ORDER BY version DESC LIMIT 1 — store/plan.go:396-407 GetLatestPlanForTraderSession, called trader/auto_trader_reread.go:58. Budget: store.GetReplanBudget store/strategy.go:1268-1281 → system_config keys ResetBaselineKey 'dayplan_reset:<trader>:<date>:<session>' (store/strategy.go:1319-1321) and ReplansUsedKey 'dayplan_replans_used:<trader>:<date>:<session>:b<baseline>' (store/strategy.go:1240-1242), read by readReplansUsed store/strategy.go:1252-1264.

A failed POST toasts (RereadButton.tsx:67-69) and the confirm sheet recovers (busy reset in `finally`, :73-75).
- **notes** A29: gate.replan_cap and gate.session and gate.version (trader/auto_trader_reread.go:27,:30,:31) production call sites in RereadButton: 0 — only `allowed`, `reason`, `replans_left` are read. Not wired to autoRefresh.ts (grep -i plan web/src/lib/autoRefresh.ts → 0 hits): raw setInterval, so it does NOT pause on document.hidden the way useAutoRefresh promises (autoRefresh.ts:17-21).

**8. Plan availability — night / no-plan-yet / error state panels**

- **component** web/src/components/plan/SessionPlanCard.tsx:48-83 StatePanel; error branch :218-227; loading :228-230; night :258-269; no-plan-yet :271-280; no-doc :284-295
- **hook/client** usePlanToday usePlan.ts:17-40; `errored` is derived in PlanCard.tsx:168 as `!!error && !plan`
- **endpoint** `GET /api/plan/today`
- **handler** api/handler_plan.go:288 — the found=false payload is built at api/handler_plan.go:352-361 and returned at :374 (night/disabled), :378 (explicit unknown session), :382 (no row), :411 (doc unmarshal failure)
- **store/frame** night ← reg.IsNightMode(now) api/handler_plan.go:310 → kernel/session_registry.go:214; runnableOK ← at.SessionRunnable trader/auto_trader_planconfig.go:126; hasRow ← store/plan.go:396 SELECT on `plans`
- **fields** plan.found, plan.night, plan.session, plan.doc; icons/strings tp('night'|'nightHint'|'noPlanYet'|'noPlanYetHint'|'errorFailClosed'|'errorHint'|'loading')
- **cadence** 15_000 ms (usePlan.ts:13,:34)
- **on failure** THE IMPORTANT ONE. httpClient.request returns {success:false} for any HTTP error WITH a response body (web/src/lib/httpClient.ts:246-254) → getPlanToday returns null (plan.ts:274) → SWR `error` is NEVER set → `errored` false (PlanCard.tsx:168) → the card falls to `!plan || !plan.session` and renders the 🌙 NIGHT panel (SessionPlanCard.tsx:258-269). A 500 from the plan handler renders as 'Night — the bot is asleep'. Only a NETWORK error (no response) throws (httpClient.ts:258) and reaches the ⛔ panel — and only if no plan was ever loaded, because SWR retains the last data, in which case a dead backend renders a STALE plan silently.
- **notes** [A] traced, not inferred: httpClient.ts:243-259 is the only branch that decides throw-vs-resolve; plan.ts:274 discards {success:false}; usePlan.ts:39 returns `plan: data ?? null` and `error` straight from SWR.

- **on failure** plan null → SessionPlanCard's early returns fire and the chip is never rendered. weekly null (no row for the governing Monday) → the grey 'WEEKLY none' chip (WeeklyChip.tsx:26) — indistinguishable from 'the weekly read failed'. So readReplansUsed returns 0 for every chain and replans_left == replan_cap always. Concretely: chain 2026-09-04:NY:8d5c8af5_… has 6 versions and a resolved cap of 4 (strategies.id=a5b7662e-… sessions[].replan_cap=4), and the card would say '4 re-reads left' and, on v1 (the no_trade row, trigger planner_fail_closed), 'after 0 of 4 re-plans were spent and the last one died' (string web/src/i18n/plan-translations.ts:271).

**18. Version history — 🕘 historical banner (viewing a superseded version)**

- **component** web/src/components/plan/SessionPlanCard.tsx:569-654 (data-testid='historical-banner'; 'back-to-active' :590; 'death-reason' :615; 'version-diff' :635)
- **hook/client** usePlanToday keyed on version (usePlan.ts:26,:30) + usePlanVersions (usePlan.ts:44); version pin state PlanCard.tsx:45,:172
- **endpoint** `GET /api/plan/today?...&version=<n> (plan.ts:269) for the doc; GET /api/plan/versions for the metadata`
- **handler** api/handler_plan.go:288 (historical branch :393-408, `historical` emitted :475, `latest_version` :476, `created_at` :478); api/handler_plan.go:626 handlePlanVersions for death_reason / death_condition / diff_vs_next
- **store/frame** SELECT … FROM plans WHERE plan_id=? AND version=? — store/plan.go:348-358, called api/handler_plan.go:400. Metadata list: store/plan.go:475-483, called api/handler_plan.go:652. death_reason = the SUCCESSOR row's trigger_reason (api/handler_plan.go:691-695); diff_vs_next = planDocDiff over the two docs (api/handler_plan.go:696, :715-764).
- **fields** plan.historical (gate), plan.version (:585), latestVersion (:591 back button), rec.death_reason + rec.superseded_by (:613-624), rec.death_condition (:625-632), rec.diff_vs_next joined by ' · ' (:633-642), rec.created_at RAW (:647), plan.replans_left ?? 0 (:648)
- **cadence** the pinned version does NOT poll — usePlan.ts:34 sets refreshInterval to 0 whenever `version` is set. The versions list still polls at 30_000 ms (usePlan.ts:15,:56).
- **on failure** a bad ?version= → the handler 400s (api/handler_plan.go:396) or 404s (:402) → getPlanToday returns null (plan.ts:274) → no `error` → the card renders the 🌙 NIGHT panel instead of saying the version is missing. If /plan/versions fails, versions=[] → the inner IIFE at :608-610 returns null and the banner renders with only the title + hint: no death reason, no death condition, no diff, no created_at, no replans-left line — a silently truncated banner.
- **notes** [A] `rec.created_at` is rendered RAW at SessionPlanCard.tsx:647 — the Go side emits row.CreatedAt as a time.Time (api/handler_plan.go:671), i.e. RFC3339Nano, and the stored values carry a +00:00 offset (e.g. 2026-09-04 17:10:35.724026458+00:00). No CT conversion, no formatting: the 'written at' line prints a UTC timestamp, which is the same +5/6h class the mini chart fixed in e96a2bf7 ('ONE CT label site'). [A] `plan.replans_left ?? 0` at :648 renders a hard 0 when the field is absent — a plausible zero on a budget line (A24).

**19. Version history — ☠ death history block**

- **component** web/src/components/plan/SessionPlanCard.tsx:660-703 (data-testid='death-history', rows 'death-row-v<n>'); deadVersions derived :327
- **hook/client** usePlanVersions usePlan.ts:44-63 → api.getPlanVersions plan.ts:279-291
- **endpoint** `GET /api/plan/versions?trader_id=&session=[&trade_date=]`
- **handler** api/handler_plan.go:626 handlePlanVersions (route api/server.go:498-502)
- **store/frame** SELECT * FROM plans WHERE trade_date=? AND session=? AND strategy_id=? ORDER BY version ASC — store/plan.go:475-483, called api/handler_plan.go:652. is_latest = last index (api/handler_plan.go:674); death_reason = rows[i+1].TriggerReason (api/handler_plan.go:691-695).
- **fields** deadVersions = versions.filter(v => !v.is_latest) (:327); header tp('deathHistory',{n: deadVersions.length}) = 'This session already lost {n} plan(s)' (web/src/i18n/plan-translations.ts:275-278); each row 'v{n} {death_reason || lifecycle}' (:695-696); hint tp('deathHistoryHint')
- **cadence** 30_000 ms (usePlan.ts:15,:56)
- **on failure** getPlanVersions null → versions=[] (usePlan.ts:59) → deadVersions=[] → THE WHOLE BLOCK DISAPPEARS with no error. A card that lost its versions feed is visually identical to a session with no deaths.
- **notes** [A] the block over-claims on live data. On chain 2026-09-04:NY:8d5c8af5_… (n=6 versions) deadVersions = v1..v5, so the header reads 'This session already lost 5 plan(s)' — but the actual rows are v1 no_trade (planner_fail_closed), v2..v5 active, and their rendered 'death_reason' values are the SUCCESSOR's authoring trigger: v1→'owner_reset', v2→'level_event', v3→'level_event', v4→'level_event', v5→'level_event'. Zero of those five versions died; four were superseded by ordinary level-event re-reads. Since D3 (store/plan.go:292-294) trigger_reason is defined as the AUTHORING trigger, so 'successor's trigger_reason == why the predecessor died' (api/handler_plan.go:685-688) no longer holds. The same string feeds the chip tooltips (SessionPlanCard.tsx:343-349).

**21. Plan mini chart (candles + level overlay + no-levels caption)**

- **component** web/src/components/plan/PlanMiniChart.tsx:56 (mounted SessionPlanCard.tsx:749-754, inside `exchange === 'ninjatrader'` at :747); caption SessionPlanCard.tsx:755-766 (data-testid='mini-chart-no-levels')
- **hook/client** raw httpClient.request({silent:true}) at PlanMiniChart.tsx:154-162 — no SWR hook, no api client method
- **endpoint** `GET /api/klines?symbol=&interval=5m&limit=1500&exchange=ninjatrader — PlanMiniChart.tsx:153 (bars only; LEVELS are props, never fetched — PlanMiniChart.tsx:2-3)`
- **handler** api/handler_klines.go:23 handleKlines (route api/server.go:156); ninjatrader branch api/handler_klines.go:70-76 → getKlinesFromNinjaTrader api/handler_klines.go:345-360
- **store/frame** NOT SQL. market.FuturesBarsProvider (declared market/futures_data.go:14) is bound to the live NT8 BarCache at trader/ninjatrader/bars_market_bridge.go:18-31 — `server.BarCache().Get(symbol, timeframe)` over the TCP bar frames, converted by barsToKlines. Levels come from plan.level_facts (api/handler_plan.go:497, computed planLevelFacts api/handler_plan.go:538-612 over the same provider).
- **fields** per bar: openTime, open, high, low, close (PlanMiniChart.tsx:155-160, mapped :170-177, deduped/sorted :178-179, pushed :180). Overlay: facts[].price, .label, .grade, .instruction only (factsToOverlay PlanMiniChart.tsx:39-46). CT axis labels via ctTickMarkFormatter / ctCrosshairTimeFormatter (PlanMiniChart.tsx:106,:108). Caption tp('chartNoLevels') when facts.length===0.
- **cadence** 15_000 ms — PlanMiniChart.tsx:192 `const id = setInterval(load, 15_000)`. Raw setInterval; NOT wired to web/src/lib/autoRefresh.ts (grep -i plan over that file: 0 hits), so no skip-if-in-flight and no pause on document.hidden (the guarantees autoRefresh.ts:17-21 claims for polling surfaces).
- **on failure** THREE distinct silent modes. (a) fetch throws → `catch { /* silent */ }` PlanMiniChart.tsx:187-189 → the chart keeps the PREVIOUS bars: stale candles, no indicator. (b) NT8 provider unbound or cache cold → api/handler_klines.go:350-358 returns HTTP 200 with `[]` → res.success true → setData([]) → a BLANK chart with no message (the 'no levels' caption at SessionPlanCard.tsx:755 only covers missing LEVELS, not missing BARS). (c) no 2d canvas / createChart throws → setFailed(true) (PlanMiniChart.tsx:82-84, :141-143) → the placeholder renders tp('loading') (PlanMiniChart.tsx:217) — a permanently failed chart displays 'Loading…' forever.
- **notes** The container is aria-hidden (PlanMiniChart.tsx:231). facts are shared with the ZoneTable (SessionPlanCard.tsx:305 `const facts = plan.level_facts ?? []`), so a level-facts failure blanks the overlay and the table together. A29: PlanLevelFact fields touch_state, sweep, closes_beyond, accept_have, accept_need, still_valid, machine_grade, origin, note, scenario_id are emitted (api/handler_plan.go:577-608) but the chart reads none of them (PlanMiniChart.tsx:40-45) — they belong to the ZoneTable group.

*14 panels.*


**1. AlertCenter (bell + badge + P0 banner + feed dropdown)**

- **store/frame** table day_plan_alerts. Feed: AlertStore.List store/alert.go:74-85 — `WHERE trader_id = ? AND dismissed = false ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 50`. Badge: AlertStore.UnackedCount store/alert.go:88-93 — `WHERE trader_id=? AND acked=false AND dismissed=false`, UNCAPPED.

- **store/frame** expectancy.LoadAndBuildAt(s.store.GormDB(), time.Now()) at api/handler_expectancy.go:39. Four raw SQL reads: expectancy/aggregate.go:112-122 `SELECT … FROM trader_positions p LEFT JOIN plans pl ON pl.plan_id=p.plan_id AND pl.version=p.plan_version WHERE p.status='CLOSED' AND p.entry_time >= ?` bound to store.DayPlanEraStart (store/attribution.go:146, resolved 2026-08-15 00:00 CT = 1786770000000); :138-139 `SELECT … FROM armed_orders WHERE state='filled'`; :159-160 `SELECT … FROM trade_excursions`; :175-177 `SELECT … FROM ab_confirm_log`. Aggregation expectancy/aggregate.go:185 BuildAt.
- **fields** HEADER: data.by (:154), data.as_of_utc sliced to 10 (:156). PER ROW: label(r.key) joining condition·session·level_kind·path·era (:87-90, :205), r.n (:213), r.wins/r.losses (:219), r.mean (:228, sign drives colour :225), r.mean_lo…r.mean_hi (:234), r.win_rate as % (:240), r.median_mae 1dp (:247), r.descriptive_only ? 'DESCRIPTIVE ONLY' : r.status (:262), r.row_ids.length as the toggle label (:270) and r.row_ids.join(', ') expanded (:282) with r.excluded_unresolved (:283-284). LEDGER: data.excluded.unresolved_pnl / .unresolvable / .test_seam / .no_condition, data.min_n, data.promotion_rule (:299-302). E8: c.key.condition, c.key.session, c.rule, c.n, c.usable_n, c.wins, c.losses, c.mean, c.excluded_price_scale, c.excluded_zero_pnl, c.short_suspect (:325-340).
- **cadence** 60 s — raw `setInterval(load, 60_000)` at ExpectancyPanel.tsx:106. Not SWR, not autoRefresh.ts.
- **on failure** THREE DISTINCT BEHAVIOURS, one of them a white-screen. (a) TRANSPORT failure / non-2xx: getExpectancy returns null (plan.ts:583) → :103 `if (alive && res) setData(res)` never fires → :113 `if (!data) return null` → the ENTIRE panel is absent, silently. (b) BUILD failure: the Go handler returns HTTP **200** with `{error, rows:[], excluded, min_n}` and NO `by`, NO `as_of_utc`, NO `promotion_rule`, NO `counterfactual_e8` (api/handler_expectancy.go:43-48). `res` is truthy → setData succeeds → the collapsed toggle renders "Expectancy · by undefined (as of —)" (:154-156), and the `error` string is NEVER rendered anywhere. (c) On OPENING that panel: `data.counterfactual_e8.length` at ExpectancyPanel.tsx:305 dereferences undefined → TypeError. There is NO error boundary above it (PlanCard.tsx:97 is bare; PlanErrorBoundary's only production call site is AskPlannerPanel.tsx:464) → the whole React tree white-screens. Genuine-empty renders :170-172 "No closed trades resolve to a play yet — nothing to measure. This is an empty sample, not a zero result." — the SAME text the build-failure case (b) produces, which makes it a fabricated claim in that branch.
- **notes** REPRODUCED FROM THE STORE (A21/A22, era bound 1786770000000, exclusion order per expectancy/aggregate.go:187-205): trader_positions status='CLOSED' total = 587; loaded (entry_time >= era) = 71; the other 516 are ABSENT not excluded, matching Exclusions.CryptoEra documented ALWAYS 0 at expectancy/model.go:191-194 and the 516 figure in store/attribution.go:141. Of the 71: excluded.test_seam = 3 (source='e7_farside_test', expectancy/model.go:60 + aggregate.go:189-191); excluded.unresolvable = 7 (plan_id='UNRESOLVABLE', store/attribution.go:25 + aggregate.go:197-200); survivors = 61, all 61 join a plan doc and all 61 carry a non-empty cited_scenario_id; of those, pnl_corrected IS NULL for 3 → excluded.unresolved_pnl = 3 (counted, kept as a rec, never in a statistic — aggregate.go:224-230). So at most 58 rows carry money, spread across condition × session × level_kind × path × era, against MinN = 30 (expectancy/model.go:27) — which is why PlanCard.tsx:95-96 says almost every row reads DESCRIPTIVE ONLY. A29 — declared in ExpectancyPanel's own Cell/Payload interfaces and rendered by NOBODY: flats (:29), sum_pnl_corrected (:28), sd (:30), wilson_lo (:32), wilson_hi (:33), t_stat (:36), median_mfe (:38), stop_hit_share (:39), target_hit_share (:40), excluded.crypto_era (:73) — production call sites 0 each. Served by the handler but absent from the TS type entirely: as_of_ms (handler_expectancy.go:68), built_at_ms (:70), era_0b_start (:71) — production call sites 0. E8Cell.counterfactual (:59) and E8Cell.note (:61) — 0. NOTE on the excluded ledger: crypto_era is the one honest omission (always 0 by construction), but the panel's own comment at :292-293 ("An excluded row that is not counted is a row that silently shrank the denominator") is violated by nothing here — the four rendered counters are the four that can be non-zero.

- **store/frame** trader/auto_trader_reread.go:36-81 CanForceReread. Reads, in order: at.dayPlanEnabled() (:37), reg.ActiveSession (:40), at.sessionRunnable (:44), kernel.IsCMEOpen (:52), then table `plans` via at.store.Plan().GetLatestPlanForTraderSession(tradeDate, sess.Name, at.id) (:57), then the RECORDED budget store.GetReplanBudget(at.store, at.id, tradeDate, sess.Name, cap) (:65) — the class-35 counter in system_config, the same seam the death gate reads.

Static: tp('rereadTitle') (:105), tp('rereadRunning') (:102), tp('rereadConfirmHint') (:137).
- **cadence** 30 s — raw `setInterval(load, 30_000)` at RereadButton.tsx:42. Plus an immediate re-fetch after every POST (RereadButton.tsx:71-72). Not SWR, not autoRefresh.ts.

The only `replan_cap` reads in web/src are the Strategy-Studio CONFIG editor (web/src/components/strategy/DayPlanEditor.tsx:498,838,848) and SessionPlanCard.tsx:338-341, which reads plan.replan_cap off the /plan/today payload — a different field from a different endpoint. RereadGate.session (plan.ts:236): 0. RereadGate.version (plan.ts:239): 0. So the confirm sentence says only "N left" and never "N of CAP", despite the cap being on the wire (auto_trader_reread.go:69). The refusal strings the owner actually sees are authored at trader/auto_trader_reread.go:38, :42, :48, :52, :59, :74, :77 — all human-language, none silent.

**8. ArmedUnderBlock (position/plan provenance — "armed under v2, plan now v3")**

- **component** web/src/components/plan/ArmedUnderBlock.tsx:29, mounted web/src/components/plan/SessionPlanCard.tsx:884-890 (between ScenarioList and RulesBlock)
- **hook/client** usePlanToday web/src/components/plan/usePlan.ts:17-40 → planApi.getPlanToday web/src/lib/api/plan.ts:257-275; the field is reached by an inline cast at SessionPlanCard.tsx:886-887 `(plan as { open_position?: OpenPositionProvenance | null }).open_position` — it is NOT declared on the PlanToday interface (web/src/lib/api/plan.ts:82-…)
- **endpoint** `GET /api/plan/today (rides the plan payload as `open_position`) — no endpoint of its own`
- **handler** api/handler_plan.go:473 `"open_position": s.openPositionProvenance(traderID, row.Version)` → api/handler_plan_position_provenance.go:20 openPositionProvenance
- **store/frame** s.store.Position().GetOpenPositions(traderID) at api/handler_plan_position_provenance.go:24 — table trader_positions, status OPEN. Only `opens[0]` is used (:28). Fields come off that row: p.Symbol/.Side/.EntryPrice/.Quantity/.CitedScenarioID/.PlanVersion (:30-38, :43-48). `live_plan_version` is the plan row's own Version, passed in from handler_plan.go:473.
- **fields** Guarded by `if (!position || !position.version_differs) return null` (:36). Then: armed_under_version as `v<n>` else armed_under_note else the literal 'version not recorded' (:42-45, printed :66) · cited_scenario_id or '—' (:39, :66) · side lower-cased (:38, :66) · entry_price 2dp when > 0 (:67-69) · quantity when > 0 (:70-72) · live_plan_version or '—' (:81) · static tp('armedUnder') (:62) and tp('planNow') (:77).
- **cadence** 15 s — PLAN_REFRESH_MS = 15_000 web/src/components/plan/usePlan.ts:13, applied as refreshInterval at usePlan.ts:34. Pinned to 0 (no polling) when a HISTORICAL version is being viewed (usePlan.ts:34 `version ? 0 : PLAN_REFRESH_MS`).
- **on failure** RENDERS NOTHING, ALWAYS SILENTLY. Three separate paths collapse to the same null: (a) getPlanToday fails → returns null (plan.ts:274) → SessionPlanCard takes its error/no-plan StatePanel branch, this block is never reached; (b) the handler's helper returns nil on a store error OR when flat — `if err != nil || len(opens) == 0 { return nil }` (handler_plan_position_provenance.go:25-27) — a READ ERROR and a FLAT ACCOUNT are indistinguishable on the wire; (c) version_differs false → :36 returns null. There is no error state and no "could not read positions" surface.
- **notes** UNREACHABLE FALLBACK. `armed_under_note` = "version not recorded" is set ONLY when p.PlanVersion == 0 (api/handler_plan_position_provenance.go:45-48), but `version_differs` requires `p.PlanVersion > 0` (handler_plan_position_provenance.go:38). So whenever the note is set, version_differs is false, and ArmedUnderBlock.tsx:36 returns null before the note at :45 can render. The branch is DEAD BY CONSTRUCTION — production reachability 0 — and it is exactly the pre-attribution case the handler's comment at :40-42 says the card must speak up about. MEASURED that the class is real: of the 71 CLOSED post-era positions, 8 carry plan_version = 0 (ids in 545..580) — 11.3%; had any been open, the card would have said nothing. SECOND: `opens[0]` at :28 silently picks ONE position — under the multi-symbol MNQ+ES fan-out a second open position is invisible. A29: `position.symbol` is served (:30) and declared (ArmedUnderBlock.tsx:18) and rendered NOWHERE — production call sites 0, which also means the block never says WHICH instrument it is talking about. CURRENT STATE: all 587 rows in trader_positions are status='CLOSED', 0 OPEN — so openPositionProvenance returns nil and this block is dark right now.

**9. RulesBlock / NoTradeBand (no-trade windows + invalidation / death condition)**

- **component** web/src/components/plan/RulesBlock.tsx:39, mounted web/src/components/plan/SessionPlanCard.tsx:893-898
- **hook/client** usePlanToday web/src/components/plan/usePlan.ts:17-40; the band is reached by an inline cast at SessionPlanCard.tsx:895 `(plan as { no_trade_band?: NoTradeBandWindow[] }).no_trade_band` — like open_position, NOT declared on the PlanToday interface
- **endpoint** `GET /api/plan/today (rides the plan payload as `no_trade_band` + `doc.no_trade` + `doc.death_condition`) — no endpoint of its own`
- **handler** api/handler_plan.go:496 `"no_trade_band": kernel.RenderNoTradeBand(&doc, sess, now)` → kernel/no_trade_band.go:213-227 RenderNoTradeBand → kernel/no_trade_band.go:~126 EvaluateNoTradeWindows (status assignment at kernel/no_trade_band.go:154-162). `doc` (no_trade prose, death_condition) is emitted at api/handler_plan.go:490.
- **store/frame** NOT a query — computed at read time from the stored plan doc. doc.NoTradeWindows comes off the `plans` row's `doc` JSON column (loaded in handlePlanToday and resolved through overlays); the window set is built by kernel.BuildMachineNoTradeWindows (kernel/no_trade_band.go:84) plus T1 blackouts re-resolved at read time (kernel/no_trade_band.go:181-195 T1NoTradeWindowsFromCT). Status is judged against the READER's CT clock (kernel/no_trade_band.go:225-226).
- **fields** live windows (status==='live', filter :59) via windowText → w.label, or `label (start_ct–end_ct CT)` when the label does not already contain start_ct (:34-37), joined by ' · ' (:93) · tp('noTradeNoneLive') when the band exists but nothing is live (:94) · doc.no_trade prose joined (:91) ONLY when there is no band (proseIsRules, :65) · a collapsed count of spent windows with w.status mapped to tp('bandElapsed')/tp('bandOtherSession') (:115-129) · a collapsed "Model notes (N)" disclosure of the prose when a band DOES exist (:136-163) · deathCondition (:174) under tp('planDies') (:172).
- **cadence** 15 s — PLAN_REFRESH_MS = 15_000 web/src/components/plan/usePlan.ts:13 / :34. Status is re-judged server-side on every one of those reads (kernel/no_trade_band.go:224-226), so an elapsing window demotes itself within 15 s.
- **on failure** RENDERS THE MODEL'S PROSE AS LIVE RULES. RenderNoTradeBand returns nil for a doc with no machine windows (kernel/no_trade_band.go:216) or an unparseable session window (kernel/no_trade_band.go:218-221) — silently, no marker on the wire. RulesBlock.tsx:65 then sets `proseIsRules = !hasBand` and :90-91 prints doc.no_trade verbatim under the red NO-TRADE header. That is precisely the ASIA-at-23:00 failure the file's own header (RulesBlock.tsx:4-8) exists to stop. Whole-block absence only when `!hasBand && prose.length===0 && !deathCondition` (:66). A failed /plan/today never reaches here (SessionPlanCard's errored StatePanel intercepts).
- **notes** MEASURED — THE FALLBACK IS THE MAJORITY CASE. `plans` holds 254 rows; only 26 contain `no_trade_windows` in the doc (10.2%). The band first appears 2026-09-03 05:08:45Z; the 228 rows without it span 2026-08-16 00:44:31Z → 2026-09-04 13:11:45Z. Note the LAST band-less row is recent and is a fail-closed one: plan_id 2026-09-04:NY v1, lifecycle `no_trade`, band=N — so the fail-closed write path still produces prose-as-rules, matching the caveat at kernel/no_trade_band.go:209-211. Every 2026-09-04 v2..v6 row does carry the band. A29: NoTradeBandWindow.source (RulesBlock.tsx:27, served kernel/no_trade_band.go:71 `json:"source"`, values SourceCodeConstant / SourceCalendar) — production call sites 0, so a hard red-news T1 blackout and a code-constant lunch window are visually identical on the card. NoTradeBandWindow.end_min (RulesBlock.tsx:22, kernel/no_trade_band.go:68): used only in a React key at :124, never rendered — 0. STALENESS NOTE: the newest plan row is 2026-09-04T17:10:35Z; today is 2026-09-06, so the live card is currently in a no-plan/night state and this block is dark.

Production call sites: 0. The only `kpi` occurrences in web/src outside tests are the three inside plan.ts itself. Worse, the TS type is already behind the Go one: VerdictStats.Declined (store/plan_qa.go:176) — the entire W16/R2 decline signal, the fix whose commit message says the KPI "lost half its signal" — is not even declared in SycophancyKPI. SECOND A29 SET, on PlanQAMessage: trader_id, plan_id, trade_date, session, context_type, trigger, challenge_type, cost_usd, latency_ms are all persisted (store/plan_qa.go:20-49) and none is declared on the TS PlanQAMessage (plan.ts:740-750) — production call sites 0 each, despite store/plan_qa.go:44-48 stating cost/latency exist "so the owner can see what the automation spends". `created_at` IS declared (plan.ts:749) but never rendered — 0.

- **fields** same rendered set as 10a, plus the header chip which here has planVersion but still no planId, so it renders `v<plan.version>` only (AskPlannerPanel.tsx:432-434)

Production call sites for the planId prop: 0. Consequence: when the owner opens 💬 while viewing a HISTORICAL version through the version chips, the banner correctly says tp('askContextHistorical') (SessionPlanCard.tsx:949) while the THREAD loaded underneath is TODAY'S ACTIVE plan's, because the client omits &plan_id and api/handler_plan.go:1513-1522 resolves the live one. The label and the content disagree. The header chip likewise can never render its plan-id half (:433). MEASURED, the whole Ask surface is nearly cold: plan_qa n=9 rows, ids 1..9, across 4 distinct plan_ids — 3 owner rows, and 6 planner rows whose verdict is DEFEND in every single case (3 with trigger='', 2 'manual-realign', 1 'owner-edit'). ZERO CONCEDE, ZERO PROPOSE-MERGE. So the Apply / Keep-as-is buttons at AskPlannerPanel.tsx:243-296 — gated on `m.verdict === 'PROPOSE-MERGE' && m.patch` (:243) — have NEVER been reachable in production, and neither has PatchPreview (:45). Total spend across all 9 rows: $0.0008; mean latency 8,838 ms.
