# Appendix — all 106 panel-group surprises (A23: reported, not acted on)

[1] DEAD COMPONENTS — four of the seven files in web/src/components/charts/ are never mounted. ChartWithOrders.tsx (669 lines), ChartWithOrdersSimple.tsx (121), ComparisonChart.tsx (578), TradingViewChart.tsx (421): production call sites 0 each. Verified by grepping web/src for both `<Name` and the bare identifier; only surviving mentions are comments at web/src/utils/traderColors.ts:2 and web/src/lib/chartTime.ts:13. There is no index barrel in that directory. ChartWithOrders.tsx was named in the dispatch's WHERE-TO-START list — it renders no panel on the running binary.

[2] CROSS-ACCOUNT MARKER LEAK (measured) — AdvancedChart.tsx:463 fetches /api/positions/history WITHOUT &account=, so api/handler_order.go:235 passes c.Query("account")=="" and store/position.go:789-791 skips the account filter. Of the top 200 CLOSED rows by exit_time (n=200), 127 are «SIM-A» (ids 393..591) and 73 are «SIM-B» (ids 382..519). Those 73 render as B/S markers on the chart no matter which account is selected.

[3] OPEN POSITIONS ARE NEVER MARKED — store/position.go:788 hard-filters status='CLOSED', so AdvancedChart.tsx:479's entry-marker branch can never fire for a live position. The FE code at :495 that checks status==='CLOSED' for the exit implies the author expected OPEN rows too. Store: 587 CLOSED rows, 0 OPEN.

[4] WORKING-LINE MISPLACEMENT — for a resting limit entry, trader/class33_boot_sweep.go:155-165 emits Type='LIMIT' with Price=EntryPx and StopPrice=StopPx. AdvancedChart.tsx:1072-1073 prefers stop_price while :1081/:1094-1096 classifies by type, so the panel draws ONE yellow line at the PROTECTIVE STOP labelled `Limit BUY 1` and never draws the entry. Currently unobservable: armed_orders rows in ('armed','working','cancel_pending') = 0 (present states: cancelled 51, filled 10, superseded 6).

[5] LEDGER-BACKED /api/open-orders IGNORES ?symbol= — trader/class33_boot_sweep.go:137-168 never reads its `symbol` argument (it stamps at.futuresSymbol() instead), so the FE's per-symbol filter at AdvancedChart.tsx:536 is decorative. Also the NT8 broker order book (nt8_order_snapshots, store/nt8_order_snapshot.go:39) is NOT the source for this panel despite existing.

[6] PLAUSIBLE ZERO in the equity header — EquityChart.tsx:275 renders `account?.total_equity.toFixed(2) || '0.00'`. The /api/account SWR has NO error branch anywhere in this component (the error card at :106 watches only the equity-history SWR), so a failed or pending account call renders a confident **0.00 USD**.

[7] FABRICATED CYCLE NUMBERS — EquityChart.tsx:192 `cycle: point.cycle_number ?? index + 1`. api/handler_competition.go:152-160 emits no cycle_number at all (grep for json:"cycle_number" hits only store/decision.go:79, store/watch_assessment.go:22, agent/tools.go:1432). The tooltip's 'Cycle #N' is ALWAYS the array index.

[8] SLIDING EQUITY BASELINE (measured) — the handler LIMITs to 10000 rows (api/handler_competition.go:140) and the FE takes validHistory[0].available_balance as the baseline (EquityChart.tsx:173). For account «SIM-A» (n=27533, ids 7559..37888) the 10000-row window currently starts at id 25394 @ 2026-08-10 10:28:22Z with balance 50828.0, while traders.initial_balance = 50000.0. The tile therefore reads 50828.00 and DRIFTS as new snapshots arrive. Worst case, if both baselines are absent, EquityChart.tsx:180 hard-defaults to 1000.

[9] STALE EQUITY SERIES — the newest trader_equity_snapshots row for «SIM-A» is id 37888 @ 2026-09-04 18:27:48Z (total_equity 51906.5), i.e. ~2 days old as of 2026-09-06. The header (live TCP frame, 15 s) and the footer 'Current Equity' tile (DB snapshot, 30 s) are two different sources for the same concept with no on-screen indication when they diverge.

[10] TIMEZONE CONTRACT BREACH — AdvancedChart.tsx:1538 formats the OHLC tooltip header with toLocaleString and NO timeZone option (browser-local), while the x-axis (:612) and crosshair (:627) both use the CT-pinned helpers. That is exactly what web/src/lib/chartTime.ts:1-14 forbids ('no component may do its own timezone math').

[11] CHART INDICATOR PERIODS ARE HARDCODED AND DIVERGE FROM THE AI — MA5/10/20/60, EMA12/26 and BB(20,2) are fixed literals at AdvancedChart.tsx:168-203 and web/src/utils/indicators.ts:68-69, computed CLIENT-SIDE. They are not the strategy's configured IndicatorPeriods, so the chart overlay and the AI prompt can show different EMA/BOLL curves for the same bars.

[12] EMA CRASH PATH — web/src/utils/indicators.ts:43-47 indexes data[i] for i<period with no length guard. Fewer bars than the period (cold NT8 cache + EMA26 on) throws inside loadData, is caught at AdvancedChart.tsx:1026 and surfaces as a red overlay carrying a raw JS TypeError string.

[13] MARKET-TYPE PILLS ARE BROKEN FOR FUTURES — ChartTabs.tsx:229-230 changes marketType and the symbol but currentExchange (:178-181) keeps exchangeId='ninjatrader'. Clicking 'Crypto' issues /api/klines?symbol=BTCUSDT&exchange=ninjatrader → api/handler_klines.go:346 returns [] → blank chart, no error, and the only trace is a server-side Warnf.

[14] EMPTY 200 IS NOT AN ERROR ANYWHERE ON THE CHART — the most common NT8 failure (market closed / cache cold) yields [] with HTTP 200 (api/handler_klines.go:348,:353,:357), so setData([]) blanks the candles while the price readout keeps the PREVIOUS values (AdvancedChart.tsx:775/792 have no length===0 branch) and no error overlay appears.

[15] SVP CONTRACT HAS FIVE DEAD FIELDS — inVA, downVol, date, partial, frozen are declared in SessionVolumeProfile.ts:43,:44,:51,:52,:57 and emitted by kernel/svp.go:69,:70,:80,:81,:86, and read by NOBODY. production call sites: 0 each (only the interface declarations survive a repo-wide grep).

[16] SSE BAR STREAM IS UNCONSUMED — /api/v1/bars/stream (api/server.go:127) and /api/v1/bars/stream-ticket (:161) have zero FE call sites; grep for EventSource|bars/stream over web/src returns nothing. Every chart panel polls.

[17] REFRESH_EQUITY_MS (web/src/lib/autoRefresh.ts:56, =10_000) is declared and read by nobody — production call sites: 0. EquityChart hardcodes 30000 at :71 and 15000 at :81 instead, defeating the file's stated 'one tuning point' purpose.

[18] STALE COMMENT IN A LIVE HANDLER — api/handler_klines.go:343 says the auto-subscribed timeframe set is '5m/15m/1h'. provider/ninjatrader/tcp_server.go:435 has all 14 (1m,3m,5m,15m,30m,1h,2h,4h,6h,8h,12h,1d,3d,1w), matching ChartTabs.tsx:113-128 and parity-tested at provider/ninjatrader/timeframes_parity_test.go:17-26. Similarly api/handler_svp.go:51 calls 2000 'the cache cap' but the cap is 2500 (provider/ninjatrader/bar_cache.go:24).

[19] /api/klines is JWT-PROTECTED (api/server.go:156, inside the group opened at :146) but /api/equity-history is PUBLIC (api/server.go:117, ownership deliberately skipped at :792-800). Two panels of the same card sit on opposite sides of the auth boundary. A 401 on the klines call redirects the entire SPA to /login (web/src/lib/httpClient.ts:135-161) — `silent:true` does not suppress it.

[20] onSymbolChange is passed from ChartTabs.tsx:490 and immediately discarded — AdvancedChart.tsx:118-120 renames it `_onSymbolChange` and calls `void` on it. production call sites: 0.

[21] AUTHENTICATED-API LIMIT: every figure above is settled against the store plus the handler code that computes it. Nothing here rests on an /api response — token minting was blocked, so /api/health and unauthenticated /api/traders were the only endpoints I could reach. Claims that could ONLY be settled by an authenticated call (e.g. the live value of account.total_equity from the NT8 account_balance frame, and whether accountsData.current is currently '«SIM-A»') are stated as code paths, not as observed values.

[22] 402 BANNER CANNOT CLEAR THE WAY IT SAYS IT DOES [A]. TraderDashboardPage.tsx:633-634 renders 'the banner clears on the first successful call' and the comment at :181 repeats it. The banner reads /api/risk/errors -> telemetry.ErrorSummary (telemetry/errors.go:117-132) over the in-memory errorEvents map (:49). There is NO decrement, delete, ack or clear function in telemetry/errors.go — the only reset is SetErrorSessionDay (:60-68), called from trader/auto_trader_loop.go:256 at the 17:00 CT CME rollover. The advertised auto-ack (trader/auto_trader_ai402.go:71-77) calls store.Alert().AckByEvent — the ALERTS table, a surface this banner never reads. Net: after a 402 outage the red banner stays up until the session rollover or a process restart, telling the owner cycles are failing when they have recovered.

[23] 402 COUNT UNDER-REPORTS [A]. TraderDashboardPage.tsx:191 uses .find(r => r.type === 'ai_payment_402') on a list sorted by count desc (telemetry/errors.go:130). Rows are keyed by {trader,type,cause,cost} (:32-37), so two 402s with different provider text become two rows and the banner renders only the largest one's count while claiming '{count} this session'. A rate/total without its true n.

[24] positions_flattened IS A HARDCODED LITERAL [A]. api/handler_risk.go:121 sets PositionsFlattened: 1 on the live NT8 TCP path, unconditionally, after a single CloseLong("",0). The CSV path (:154) uses adapter.flattened, which handler_risk.go:55 increments exactly once — also always 1. EmergencyFlatButton renders this straight to the operator (EmergencyFlatButton.tsx:40). The field counts nothing.

[25] EMERGENCY FLAT RENDERS A REFUSAL AS GREEN SUCCESS [A]. EmergencyFlatButton.tsx:29-40 never checks r.ok; the response body is JSON.stringify'd into a <pre> whose class is text-green-300 (:92). An HTTP 400/404 (including the cross-user 404 from api/handler_plan.go:124) prints {"error":"..."} in green in the confirm modal. It also uses raw fetch with the JWT read from localStorage (:28), bypassing httpClient's interceptors, so an expired token also renders green.

[26] FORCE-FLAT KILL-SWITCH RESET ERROR IS DISCARDED [A]. api/handler_risk.go:117 `_ = kernel.MaybeForceFlat(traderID, &forceFlatSignalAdapter{writer: nil})` on the TCP path. The modal copy (EmergencyFlatButton.tsx:84-87) promises it 'triggers the kernel kill switch and resets the daily PnL window'; a failure of exactly that is invisible to caller and UI, and the response still reports triggered:true.

[27] is_current IS COMPUTED AGAINST A VALUE THE HANDLER THEN OVERWRITES [A]. api/handler_account.go:74 sets IsCurrent: a.Name == current inside the loop; lines 93-97 then reassign `current = selected` when the bound account has an account_balance snapshot. The top-level `current` and the per-row is_current flags can therefore name different accounts in the same response. Only survivable because Account.is_current (web/src/types/trading.ts:44) has production call sites: 0 — declared in the contract, read by nobody.

[28] ?account= IS HONOURED BY ONLY HALF OF /api/account [A]. api/handler_order.go:159 scopes the LEDGER by c.Query("account"), but equity/available/P&L/position_count come from trader.GetAccountInfo() at :145 -> trader/auto_trader_decision.go:117, which takes no account argument and always serves t.boundAccount (trader/ninjatrader/tcp_trader.go:792). Requesting a different ?account= returns one account's ledger stitched to another account's equity, in one JSON object, with nothing saying so.

[29] THE LEDGER CHIP IS SCOPED BY THE STREAMED ACCOUNT, NOT THE BOUND ONE [A/B]. web/src/router/AppRoutes.tsx:315 sets selectedAccount = accountsData?.current (the streamed connection account), and threads it as ?account= at :330. AccountSelector.tsx:175-184 warns the operator precisely when current !== selected — and in that exact state the LEDGER_DAY chip is computed for the wrong account and carries no warning of its own.

[30] THE ACCOUNT THE NUMBERS BELONG TO IS DROPPED BEFORE THE WIRE [A]. trader/ninjatrader/tcp_trader.go:822 returns "account": acct.Account with the explicit comment 'Lets the dashboard label/guard the balance accurately' (:819-821). trader/auto_trader_decision.go:213-230 never copies it into the /api/account map. Production call sites outside tests: 0 (only trader/ninjatrader/account_binding_test.go:127,139). The dashboard therefore cannot label whose equity it is showing.

[31] THE ACCOUNT SELECTOR NEVER POLLS [A]. Both SWR hooks on key accounts-<trader> omit refreshInterval: AccountSelector.tsx:44 and AppRoutes.tsx:313. The selected-account label, the SIM/live dropdown and the masquerade banner refresh only on mount, on trader change, or after an explicit select. The masquerade warning can therefore be wrong (in either direction) for the whole session.

[32] STALE-DATA-SILENTLY IS THE DEFAULT FAILURE MODE OF THE ACCOUNT SURFACES [A]. The four StatCards gate on `accountFailed && !account` (TraderDashboardPage.tsx:686,699,715,728) and the debug strip on `account ? … : accountFailed ? …` (:659-671). SWR retains the last good account across errors, so after ANY successful fetch the red 'DATA_FETCH::FAILED' text (translations.ts:1210) is UNREACHABLE. Meanwhile AppRoutes.tsx:332,337 sets refreshInterval to 0 for 30s at a time. The operator sees confident numbers with no staleness cue.

[33] PLAUSIBLE ZERO ON EQUITY [A]. trader/ninjatrader/tcp_trader.go:832-835 returns totalEquity 0.0 / availableBalance 0.0 when no account_balance frame has arrived, with the stated intent (:779-781) that 'the dashboard shows "no data" rather than a fabricated balance'. No such branch exists on the FE: TraderDashboardPage.tsx:688 renders '0.00' and :690 appends 'USD'. An unconnected AddOn is indistinguishable from a wiped account.

[34] THE SAME FIELD DRIVES OPPOSITE ARROWS ON TWO ADJACENT CARDS [A]. Total Equity uses positive={(account?.total_pnl ?? 0) > 0} (TraderDashboardPage.tsx:692); Total P&L uses >= 0 (:721). total_pnl exactly 0 renders a red ▼ on one card and a green ▲ on the other. Both also pass change={account.total_pnl_pct || 0} (:691,:720) — the `|| 0` turns an absent pct into a rendered '+0.00%'.

[35] THE EQUITY CARD'S PERCENTAGE IS NOT AN EQUITY CHANGE [A]. change at TraderDashboardPage.tsx:691 is total_pnl_pct, which on NT8 resolves to broker-native (realized+unrealized)/(equity−pnl)×100 (trader/auto_trader_decision.go:197-206) — a P&L ratio, not a period-over-period equity delta, rendered with a ▲/▼ that reads as one.

[36] THE PNL TOOLTIP DESCRIBES THE WRONG BRANCH [A]. TraderDashboardPage.tsx:663 titles the debug-strip PNL 'NT8-native total P&L (equity − initial balance)'. On NT8 that computation (auto_trader_decision.go:182) is REPLACED by realized+unrealized at :197-206 because brokerNativePnL is true (tcp_trader.go:829). The tooltip documents the crypto path.

[37] MARGIN ON A CME CARD IS A BINANCE FICTION [A]. margin_used_pct (rendered TraderDashboardPage.tsx:736) is Σ(|positionAmt|×markPrice)/leverage ÷ equity (auto_trader_decision.go:155-171,208-211), with leverage DEFAULTING TO 10 when the position map lacks the key (:166-169). That is not CME initial margin, and the label carries no qualifier.

[38] THE GREEN 'LIVE' DOT READS NOTHING [A]. TraderDashboardPage.tsx:455 is unconditional markup — bg-nofx-green, animate-pulse, no data binding. It pulses green for a stopped, disconnected, paused or 402-dead trader.

[39] THE TRADER'S NAME IS NEVER SHOWN [A]. selectedTrader.trader_name appears exactly once in the page, as the avatar hash seed (TraderDashboardPage.tsx:450 -> PunkAvatar.tsx:340-343). The header identifies the trader only by trader_id.slice(0,8)+'...' (:460).

[40] LAST_UPDATE IS BROWSER-LOCAL, NOT CT [A]. AppRoutes.tsx:408 new Date().toLocaleTimeString(), rendered unlabelled at TraderDashboardPage.tsx:661 alongside CT-pinned surfaces (PauseButton.tsx:42-49 pins America/Chicago). It also measures when the browser received the response, not when the AddOn sampled the account.

[41] SYSTEM_STATUS::ONLINE PROVES ONLY THAT GIN ANSWERED [A]. api/server.go:628 returns the literal "ok". It reads nothing about the NT8 socket (IsConnected), feed_status, the AI provider or the DB. Live-verified: GET /api/health -> {"revision":"ea3f33fe9ccc","status":"ok","time":null}.

[42] /api/health's `time` FIELD IS PERMANENTLY NULL [A]. api/server.go:629 reads c.Request.Context().Value("time"); grep for WithValue across api/ and main.go returns nothing. Confirmed null on the running binary. Production call sites: 0.

[43] PAUSE/RESUME FAILURES ARE SWALLOWED [A]. PauseButton.tsx:65-67 and :77-79 console.error only — no toast, no inline state. A 404 (ownership, api/handler_trader.go:947/1017) or a 400 ('no active session to pause until', :974) leaves the button looking idle and unpaused, indistinguishable from never having been clicked. The sibling close-position path does call notify.error (TraderDashboardPage.tsx:302).

[44] THE {until_ct:'HH:MM'} PAUSE FORM HAS NO UI PRODUCER [A]. api/handler_trader.go:978-996 implements a strict, DST-safe CT wall-clock parse; the only client (PauseButton.tsx:116,124,132,150) sends {minutes} or {until:'session_end'}. Production call sites: 0. Same for the pause_until_ct response field (handler_trader.go:1009) — api.pauseTrader returns Promise<void> (traders.ts:79-85).

[51] selectedAccount IS THREADED INTO THE PAGE BUT NO PANEL IN THIS GROUP READS IT. Declared TraderDashboardPage.tsx:118, destructured :136, used exactly once — forwarded to DecisionAudit at :802.

[52] THE 402 POLL HAS NO try/catch [A]. TraderDashboardPage.tsx:188-193: a network failure rejects inside `void poll()` (:194) as an unhandled rejection and the banner keeps its previous state; an HTTP error status makes httpClient.request return {success:false} with no .data, so res?.rows is undefined, .find is skipped, and setAi402(null) HIDES the banner. A backend failure reads on screen as 'no 402 today'.

[53] HARNESS LIMIT / A17 SCOPE. The authenticated API was unreachable to me (token minting blocked); /api/health and unauthenticated /api/traders were the only live endpoints. Every figure above is reproduced from the code on the worktree plus read-only SQL against data/data.db. The one claim that can ONLY be settled by an authenticated call is what /api/account and /api/accounts actually return for the bound account right now — specifically whether handler_account.go:93-97 currently sets current == selected. That is UNKNOWN, not assumed.

[54] SERVED-BUNDLE PROVENANCE CAVEAT. web/dist is untracked (git ls-files web/dist is empty) and was built 2026-09-06 06:57:23, eight minutes BEFORE the e28b604d marker commit at 07:05:30. It does contain the string ea3f33fe (GUIDE_BUILT_REV), matching the running rev, so the premise holds — but the source-to-bundle identity rests on mtime, not on git.

[55] MY WORKTREE'S HEAD MOVED UNDER ME DURING THIS READ. At my first command HEAD was 92c6d9c5 (the claim commit); by the end it was 1001d691, via 5d7cb7c0 'docs(v5-precheck): running-rev proof, claim, surprises, and Section E hunk-by-hunk read' and 1001d691 'docs(v5-precheck): owner ruling — v5 is a two-file change, hunk 13 ranked behaviour-changing, desk strip coordinated not duplicated'. A concurrent lane is committing to docs/v5-precheck-0906 in /home/hoang/nofx-v5precheck while I read it. `git diff --stat e28b604d HEAD` = docs/superpowers/reports/2026-09-06-v5-precheck.md only (+384), so NO code moved and my citations are unaffected — but two agents share one worktree, which WORKTREE LAW says should not happen. Reporting, not acting.

[56] ONE ERROR BOUNDARY IN THE ENTIRE FRONTEND, AND IT GUARDS ONE DIV. `grep -rn 'ErrorBoundary|componentDidCatch|getDerivedStateFromError' web/src` (tests excluded) returns exactly three hits, all pointing at PlanErrorBoundary used inside AskPlannerPanel.tsx:464-498. PlanCard mounts AlertCenter, GateBlocksPanel, ExpectancyPanel, InstrumentsDrawer and SessionPlanCard bare (PlanCard.tsx:83, 92, 97, 102, 161). So a render throw in any of them white-screens the whole app — which is precisely the 'June family' failure PlanErrorBoundary.tsx:4-8 was written to contain, contained in only one place.

[57] THE EXPECTANCY BUILD-ERROR PATH IS A LIVE WHITE-SCREEN. api/handler_expectancy.go:43-48 answers a build failure with HTTP 200 and a payload MISSING counterfactual_e8. ExpectancyPanel.tsx:305 then evaluates `data.counterfactual_e8.length` on undefined with no boundary above it. Before it throws, the collapsed header reads 'Expectancy · by undefined (as of —)' (:154-156) and the body claims 'No closed trades resolve to a play yet — nothing to measure. This is an empty sample, not a zero result.' (:170-172) — a fabricated claim in a failure branch, on a panel whose own docstring (:9-11) forbids exactly that. The `error` string the handler sends is rendered nowhere.

[58] NOT ONE PANEL IN THIS GROUP USES THE SHARED AUTO-REFRESH LAYER. web/src/lib/autoRefresh.ts:17-21 says useAutoRefresh exists for 'components that poll with raw setInterval' and gives them skip-if-in-flight, Page-Visibility pause, and exponential error backoff. Its production importers are AdvancedChart.tsx:24, PositionHistory.tsx:3, GridRiskPanel.tsx:2, DecisionAudit.tsx:17, MarketTicker.tsx:3, AppRoutes.tsx:38 — no plan panel. GateBlocksPanel.tsx:70, ExpectancyPanel.tsx:106, InstrumentsDrawer.tsx:80, RereadButton.tsx:42 and ResetButton.tsx:41 all use raw setInterval, so they poll a hidden tab and hammer an unreachable backend with no backoff.

[59] THE GATE-BLOCKS PANEL COUNTS THINGS THAT ARE NOT REFUSALS. telemetry/gate_blocks.go is a single map keyed (trader, gate), and three production emitters put NON-refusals into it: plan_matched (trader/auto_trader_planner.go:2734, a positive outcome), arm_authored (trader/auto_trader_planner.go:1790), weekly_counter (telemetry/weekly.go:37); clock_skew_observed is documented as explicitly non-blocking at telemetry/gate_blocks.go:47-52 yet has a first-class label at GateBlocksPanel.tsx:30. The panel renders all of them in a red count chip under a header the i18n string calls refusals ('No entries refused so far this session.', plan-translations.ts:507).

[60] THE INSTRUMENTS-DRAWER HEADER COMMENT IS STALE AGAINST ITS OWN STORE. InstrumentsDrawer.tsx:5-6 justifies collapsing the drawer partly because 'MAE/MFE has no rows at all'. trade_excursions holds 587 rows and touch_outcomes holds 677 (touch_episodes 1315). The wave that filled them is store/position.go's most recent commit, 455dce5a 2026-09-05 'the touch recorder stops fabricating episodes; the exit cause is recorded, not inferred' — which IS in the running binary.

[61] THE MATCHED-RANDOM NUMBERS UNDER A PER-TRADER PANEL ARE PROCESS-WIDE. handlePlanStats takes ?trader_id=, 404s an unknown trader (api/handler_plan.go:1739-1742), and then calls CountsByType (store/matched_random.go:71-88) and LatestWeekly (store/matched_random.go:128-138), NEITHER of which has a trader_id predicate. The level-gate row therefore shows a global tally inside a trader-scoped drawer, and the 'frozen' weekly it renders is 2026-W35 computed 2026-08-30T22:01:18Z — seven days stale on 2026-09-06, with computed_at served (handler_plan.go:1748) and never displayed.

[62] THE PLAN STORE HAS NOT ADVANCED IN TWO DAYS. Newest `plans` row is 2026-09-04:NY v6 at 2026-09-04T17:10:35Z; newest day_plan_alerts row is id 655 at 2026-09-04T17:31:00Z; all 587 trader_positions rows are status='CLOSED' with zero OPEN. So on today's clock the live card is in a no-plan/night state and ArmedUnderBlock, RulesBlock and both AskPlannerPanel mounts are dark. Every store figure I quote is a reconstruction of what the handlers WOULD compute, not a reading of a live authenticated response — the authenticated API was unavailable to me as briefed.

[63] [A] CLASS-50b DUAL BIAS LABEL IS DEAD ON ARRIVAL. Go stamps `bias_label` at the PlanDoc TOP level (kernel/plan_doc.go:283, written trader/auto_trader_planner.go:1941); the FE declares it on PlanBias (web/src/lib/api/plan.ts:16) and reads `bias.bias_label` (web/src/components/plan/BiasBlock.tsx:73,:78). Store proof: of n=254 `plans` rows, 27 carry json_extract(doc,'$.bias_label') and 0 carry json_extract(doc,'$.bias.bias_label'). Sample ids 2026-09-04:NY:8d5c8af5_… v3/v4/v5/v6, value 'bias: AI short · tree short · regime up'. The line has never rendered.

[64] [A] THE DORMANT BANNER READS THE WRONG COLUMN SINCE D3. store/plan.go:287-314 stopped writing the lifecycle marker into plans.trigger_reason (comment :292-294) and appends it to plan_lifecycle_log (:306-311); SessionPlanCard.tsx:396-401 still strips a 'dormant:' prefix off trigger_reason. n=15 dormant rows: 12 pre-D3 ('dormant:%'), 3 post-D3 carrying the authoring trigger — ids 2026-09-03:ASIA:8d5c8af5_… v2, v5, v7, all 'level_event', so the banner reads 'dormant: level_event'. A29: store/plan.go:542 PlanStore.LifecycleLog production call sites: 0; no Go handler exposes plan_lifecycle_log at all.

[65] [A] THE DEATH-HISTORY BLOCK COUNTS SUPERSESSIONS AS DEATHS. death_reason = the SUCCESSOR row's trigger_reason (api/handler_plan.go:691-695), which post-D3 is an authoring trigger. On chain 2026-09-04:NY:8d5c8af5_… (n=6) the block would render 'This session already lost 5 plan(s)' (web/src/i18n/plan-translations.ts:276) with rows v1→'owner_reset', v2..v5→'level_event' — five versions of which four are lifecycle='active' and none died.

[66] [A] A BACKEND ERROR RENDERS AS '🌙 NIGHT'. httpClient.request resolves (not throws) for any HTTP error carrying a body (web/src/lib/httpClient.ts:246-254); getPlanToday discards it and returns null (plan.ts:274); SWR's `error` therefore stays undefined; PlanCard.tsx:168 computes errored=false; SessionPlanCard.tsx:258 renders the night panel. A 500 on /api/plan/today is visually identical to 'the market is closed'. On a pure network error with a previously loaded plan, SWR retains the data and the card shows a STALE plan with no marker.

[67] [A] THE TIMELINE STRIP'S ENABLED SHADING CONTRADICTS THE RUNNING BOT. sessionConfig.ts:49/:59 mirror the registry (ASIA/LONDON enabled=false — matching system_config `session_registry`), but strategies.id=a5b7662e-7bf7-49bb-9f09-7efa48f95ac8 sets day_plan.sessions ASIA.enable=true and LONDON.enable=true, and sessionRunnable lets the override win (trader/auto_trader_planconfig.go:108-113). SessionTimelineStrip.tsx:76 therefore paints ASIA/LONDON at the 'disabled' opacity 0.1 on days those sessions produce plans (rows 2026-09-03:ASIA v1..v7, 2026-09-04:LONDON v1..v3). The TABS are correct (server `runnable_sessions`); only the strip is a client mirror.

[68] [A] THE REPLAN-BUDGET COUNTER HAS NEVER RECORDED A SPEND ON THIS INSTALL. system_config rows matching 'dayplan_replans_used%': n=0. `plans` rows whose trigger_reason ∈ ('death_replan','owner_reread') — the only spend classes (store/strategy.go:1202,:1204; recorder trader/auto_trader_planner.go:1968): n=0 of 254. So replans_left == replan_cap (4) everywhere, including on the 6-version chain 2026-09-04:NY, and the NO-TRADE banner on its v1 would read 'after 0 of 4 re-plans were spent and the last one died'. This is the recorded-counter design working as specified (class 35) — not a plausible zero — but the rendered sentence is false for that chain.

[82] A24 BENIGN-STATE MASKING (highest-severity finding in this group, affects EVERY panel here). /home/hoang/nofx-v5precheck/web/src/lib/api/plan.ts:274 `return res.success && res.data ? res.data : null` swallows the failure instead of throwing. Combined with /home/hoang/nofx-v5precheck/web/src/lib/httpClient.ts:189-204, the split is: 403 / 404 / 500-without-body / network -> throws -> SWR error -> the ⛔ fail-closed panel (SessionPlanCard.tsx:218-227); but 400 / 409 / 422 / any 500 WITH a body -> Promise.reject(axiosError) -> httpClient.ts:246-254 returns {success:false} -> getPlanToday returns null -> SWR data=null with NO error -> PlanCard.tsx:168 `errored={!!error && !plan}` is false -> SessionPlanCard.tsx:231 falls through to :258 and renders '🌙 Night — The planner arms at the session open'. A rejected request is rendered as a benign night state. Additionally, if a prior poll cached a plan and a later poll throws, SWR retains `data`, so `errored` stays false and the STALE plan renders silently with no marker.

The loud ⚠️ failed-state UI only ever appears on a COLD cache.

The footer's 'Showing 193 of 200' (PositionHistory.tsx:1165-1168) attributes the whole 7-row gap to the user's filters.
